### R30 — BCPack rewrite benchmark and parity cross-check (research addendum, 2026-11-27)

**Do not reimplement R30 experiment B or confuse its saving with the decoder rewrite.** [Commit `2364a29`](https://github.com/Zaxix/VIctory/commit/2364a29a90710aeefb32d2a3677c72b85cdfcb57) already adds `bcpack::decode_into` and per-worker reusable `source`, `packed`, `records`, `bounce` scratch, plus R31 worker-count switches. This is code on `main`, not a verified Windows gameplay result; see Claude's implementation-status table for actual build/test state.

- **Exact rANS schedule is a parity gate:** [`bcpack.rs` lane loop](../crates/victory-formats/src/bcpack.rs#L305-L482) has 12 lanes (BC1/3/4/5) or 16 (BC6/7), with **at most eight input bytes per round**, conditional per-lane byte demand and lockstep output. Splitting the input round-robin by lane is invalid. The 256-block history makes final BC block reconstruction interdependent even if symbol decoding can run independently. Existing reciprocal and bucket-window lookup already optimise symbol decoding; profile Zen 3 cache pressure before adding a large lookup/SIMD design.
- **Benchmark apples to apples:** [`bcpack_throughput`](../crates/victory-formats/src/bcpack.rs#L1674-L1704) is an ignored, synthetic BC1/BC4/BC7 release test that invokes `decode()` (**fresh scratch**) on every iteration. Compare (A) old allocating baseline, (B) current `decode_into` with kept scratch, (C) proposed decoder with the **same kept scratch**, and (D) end-to-end DirectStorage. The [`dstorage::Lanes::count` BCPack timer](../crates/victory-hle/src/modules/dstorage.rs#L555-L613) wraps **whole request work** (source read, optional zlib, BCPack and output), not isolated entropy throughput. Record MB of *decoded output*/s, ns/block, allocations/capacity, stage time, queue/status delay and real frame p95 separately.
- **Correctness/independent oracle:** include all BC1/3/4/5/6/7, short/long/reset fragments, cross-eight-byte feeder transitions, >256-block history wrap, malformed and truncated inputs, trailing-byte semantics and dirty/reused scratch. The existing [`reference_chunks`](../crates/victory-formats/src/bcpack.rs#L1706-L1731) test is ignored pending external data; compare a verified independent decoder where supported. [Ahmet-Dedeler/bcpack-rs](https://github.com/Ahmet-Dedeler/bcpack-rs) includes a separate MDU rANS reference, [specification](https://github.com/Ahmet-Dedeler/bcpack-rs/blob/master/docs/BCPACK_SPEC.md) and author-reported large-corpus parity, **not tested here**; BC6H block reconstruction remains incomplete there. Some decoders reject nonzero trailing bytes while current VIctory ignores unused trailing bytes: preserve intended compatibility, do not mistake different input-validation policies for pixel-output mismatches.
- **End gate:** byte-for-byte outputs, no regressions in ordered DirectStorage status/failure semantics, Windows build and matched RTX 4070 streaming/image tests before asserting FPS. Microsoft's [GDK DirectStorage overview](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/gdk/docs/features/console/storage/directstorage/directstorage-overview) confirms the DEFLATE → BCPACK → swizzle order; Xbox BCPack is not PC GDeflate. Full Claude-ready research: `VIctory_BCPack_R30_RAM_R35_Claude_Research_Handoff_2026-11-27.txt` (local attachment, not committed).


## P0–P2 — System RAM optimisation R35–R40 (researched 2026-11-27)

**Separate from adaptive VRAM placement R23–R27.** Checked source on `main` after [`6ee65de6`](https://github.com/Zaxix/VIctory/commit/6ee65de6b93dcc2e68d07e28878901d78edf6891). README's 23–27 GB PC-memory and 9.2–9.6 GB GPU-budget figures are historical **32 GB / i7-14700KF** test observations ([diagnostics/README.md](../diagnostics/README.md)); the target stated above is a **64 GB / Ryzen 5800X** and no matching new RAM trace has been verified. The 23–27 GB is not established as process private commit, physical working set, or a leak. Keep all R35–R40 items unchecked until their gates are met.

**Already implemented — avoid duplicates:** `host::sweep()` calls `pipeline::trim_library()`, `shadow::let_go_idle()`, `texture::let_go_idle()`, `resource::let_go_idle()` every ~1 s; system-RAM idle grace is 10/5/2 seconds at ≥4 GiB / 2–4 GiB / <2 GiB free, with additional inactive-frame and reference guards ([host.rs](../crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs#L197-L297)). `shadow::memory()` already counts host copies, loose windows and pooled snapshot capacity; `texture::memory()` counts staging; `pipeline::library` uses file-backed mapping and trims its working set. D3D12 GPU-local, CPU upload and OS private allocations are not additive without ownership/accounting evidence. `VICTORY_KEEP_SNAPSHOTS=1` is off by default and historically exhausted RAM when raised; `VICTORY_STAGING_POOL` is on by default and avoids allocation stalls. Research is **not** authorization for Claude code edits through this checklist.

- [ ] **R35 — P0: Distinguish RAM categories and establish a 64 GB target baseline.** Reuse `host::pc_memory()` (currently logs global committed/free, handles and `PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS::PagefileUsage` as process private MB) but instrument exact **process private committed** (`PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS_EX.PrivateUsage`), total working set, EX2 private working set where supported, system CommitTotal/CommitLimit, hard faults, guest reserved/committed/backing, live shadow and snapshot capacities, staging active/idle/pool, mapped pipeline file resident versus mapped bytes, and DXGI LOCAL/NON_LOCAL budget and usage. Name every metric's source; `PagefileUsage` is a commit proxy, not bytes paged to disk. Use VMMap milestone snapshots and WPR heap/virtual-memory traces *separately* from low-overhead FPS A/B. Sample menu, stable world, area transition, 10 min, 30–60 min; matched SHA, scene, settings and ≥3 runs. **Done when:** a non-double-counted subsystem ledger reconciles major contributors and identifies the top two attributable private-commit or residency drivers on the actual target. Sources: [Microsoft memory definitions](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/memory/memory-performance-information), [EX2](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/psapi/ns-psapi-process_memory_counters_ex2), [VMMap](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/sysinternals/downloads/vmmap).
- [ ] **R36 — P1: Audit shadow/snapshot capacity and non-reclaimable owners.** Differentiate exact and loose shadows, CPU/card staging, active `before` snapshots, `KEPT_BYTES`, pooled Vec *capacity*, descriptor/list/fence-held resources and duplicate aliases; `shadow::POOL` keeps up to eight snapshots / 512 MiB **but always preserves the largest**, even above that total, so measure retained cost and reuse before tuning. Existing loose-window sweep requires no outstanding `Arc`, COM view or recent use. Experiment only on idle, regenerable capacities after attribution, preserving GPU-written data, guest mapping generations, coherency and outstanding recorded-but-unsubmitted lists. **Done when:** time-series ownership ledger + fault tests show a reproducible private-commit decrease without extra p95 stalls or corrupted bytes. Source: [shadow.rs pool](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs#L1703-L1740), [snapshot safety](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs#L3118-L3151), [window sweep](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs#L3305-L3386).
- [ ] **R37 — P1: Profile texture staging live/idle/pool and reuse economics.** `STAGING` and spare D3D12 upload pools already exist, with 8 MiB max per pooled item and default 64 MiB spare bound (`VICTORY_STAGING_POOL_MB`); `SPARE_LINEAR` is a distinct GPU pool, not system private bytes by definition. Distinguish table-only staging from list/flight-retained allocations and account for power-of-two padding, zeroing and re-detile/reupload on return. Controlled fresh-process A/B of modest pool caps vs baseline and `VICTORY_STAGING_POOL=0` under identical streaming workloads; count allocations, reuse, transfer cost and p95 frame time. **Done when:** retained idle bytes, savings, cache-miss/rebuild penalty and safety under burst streaming are quantified. Sources: [texture.rs pool](../crates/victory-gpu/src/texture.rs#L185-L269), [idle sweep](../crates/victory-gpu/src/texture.rs#L650-L715).
- [ ] **R38 — P1: Guest RAM virtual/physical backing and alias lifetime audit.** `XMemVirtualAlloc` uses `VirtualAlloc2`, reservation/placeholders and write-watch; physical-page remap uses section views and `MEM_COMMIT`, with an explicit Windows commit-limit diagnostic. Count reserved virtual address space separately from uniquely committed physical backing and *resident* pages; account for alias mappings, unmap/release and same-VA reuse. **Do not trim guest-canonical pages** merely for low GPU usage, or treat a nonresident committed page as absent. **Done when:** repeatable remap/alias/commit-limit tests and a non-overlapping guest-vs-host ledger confirm whether backing actually grows unexpectedly. Sources: [xmem.rs allocations](../crates/victory-hle/src/modules/xmem.rs#L87-L177), [commit-limit path](../crates/victory-hle/src/modules/xmem.rs#L1092-L1139), [VirtualFree](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/memoryapi/nf-memoryapi-virtualfree).
- [ ] **R39 — P2: Pipeline library memory-mapping and save peaks.** Existing `library.rs` already maps the cache file instead of holding ~1.3 GiB private Vec; mapped pages may leave the process working set, and a retained file handle is **mandatory** for correctness when the library is replaced. The `trim()` path exists. Review last commits [`8a3f99d`](https://github.com/Zaxix/VIctory/commit/8a3f99d27f4624876acec67338da3207ac63b219) and [`903b9c1`](https://github.com/Zaxix/VIctory/commit/903b9c1f5df6c8a02f41754291cd5220d349822f) correcting R28 save publication before proposing anything; compare mapped file size, resident pages, transient save mapping, compile worker buffers, page faults, PSO hit rate. **Done when:** any proposed trim/sharding change shows lower actual pressure without first-world stalls, cache loss or device removal. Sources: [library.rs](../crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline/library.rs), [file-mapping RAM distinctions](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/memory/working-set).
- [ ] **R40 — P2: Optional bounded RAM-pressure policy, gated by R35–R39.** Candidate only for *recreatable, unreferenced, idle* caches, with hysteresis, a limited sweep-work budget and fallback when diagnostics disagree; maintain existing >=4-frame lifetime gates, descriptor generations, CPU/GPU write order and per-queue fences. Do not use forced process working-set trimming, standby-list purges, blanket `MEM_DECOMMIT`, indiscriminate `OfferVirtualMemory`, or guest address relocation to make Task Manager report less memory. Any new `VICTORY_RAM_*` configuration names are **proposals, not current flags**. **Done when:** opt-in A/B on 64 GB target saves measured private commit/physical pressure and passes remap, concurrent CPU/GPU writes, pipeline saves, 30–60-minute endurance, image parity and release-frame p95; otherwise remain off. Sources: [D3D12 fence-based reuse](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/direct3d12/large-buffers), [D3D12 residency](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/direct3d12/residency).

**Evidence handoff to Claude:** companion local text file `VIctory_RAM_Optimization_Claude_Handoff_2026-11-27.txt` has a subsystem ledger, exact code paths, fault vectors, experiment matrix, measurement field names and Microsoft / D3D12 cross-references. **Research only; no runtime verification or claimed RAM/FPS gain.**

### R35 — DirectStorage scratch RAM and existing Windows counters (research addendum, 2026-11-27)

- **New R30 scratch is a measurable RAM owner, not a hypothetical optimisation:** [`dstorage.rs`](../crates/victory-hle/src/modules/dstorage.rs#L998-L1056) keeps up to **16 MiB per buffer** (default `VICTORY_DSTORAGE_SCRATCH_MB=16`) across four buffers for **each** storage worker; with 16 default workers on the 5800X the **theoretical maximum retained capacity is 4 × 16 × 16 MiB = 1 GiB**. This is not measured consumption or residency; allocations grow with request size and >limit buffers are dropped after each request. BC6/BC7 symbol records use **2× decoded output bytes**, so >8 MiB outputs can exceed a 16 MiB per-vector retention cap. Track each buffer's `len`, `capacity`, high water, reuse/discard counts and per-worker sums. Compare `VICTORY_DSTORAGE_SCRATCH_MB=0` against 16 under the **same** worker count to quantify allocation churn vs retained commit and frame p95.
- **Reuse existing process metrics:** regular [`host::pc_memory()`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs#L721-L747) records `PagefileUsage` (= private process commit, **not disk pagefile traffic**) and system `CommitTotal`; [`removal_memory()`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs#L932-L985) **already** reports `WorkingSetSize`, peak WS and system `CommitLimit` on device removal. Missing: comparable continuous WS/private WS/limit, robust query-error handling, aligned subsystem counters, and a baseline from the owner's real PC; do not describe these existing API calls as completely absent. [EX2 `PrivateWorkingSetSize`](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/psapi/ns-psapi-process_memory_counters_ex2) requires supported Windows versions/updates and a fallback. [Page faults](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/desktop/ETW/pagefault-typegroup1) include soft and hard; a process `PageFaultCount` does **not** isolate hard faults, for which use ETW/WPR.
- **Ledger rule:** log guest unique backing separately from aliased virtual reservations; process private commit separately from total/private resident WS; CPU scratch `Vec` capacity separately from OS committed/resident bytes; mapped pipeline cache separately from private heap; GPU LOCAL/NON_LOCAL budgets separately from process commit. Do not sum overlapping estimates and call the result RAM usage. No title data / no game run means instrumentation-only progress, not completion of R35's acceptance gate.


## P1/P2 — Owner-supplied binary fixture research (2026-11-27)

**Read-only static research**, not an instruction to modify source or a claim of tested gameplay. Input SHA-256 digests, decoded size calculations, exact codec inventory, PE imports/exports and a reproducible metadata-only Python inspector are in the **local, non-committed** `VIctory_Uploaded_Files_Reverse_Engineering_Handoff_2026-11-27.txt` and `VIctory_RDS5_Metadata_Inspector_2026-11-27.py`. No supplied game bytes, DLLs or extracted textures are to be committed. Claude's **IMPLEMENTATION STATUS — Claude-owned** near the top remains the source of truth for build/test status.

- [ ] **R30 evidence addition — real RDS5 BCPack fixtures, not an FPS result (P1).** Owner-supplied `ttdb_graphics.ttpf` (SHA-256 `0b23f3f4ea9f63d8bb8abd835fb15b584fbc63a2b2f9cdbb6f675d78db16cb7e`) is **34,411,776 physical bytes** with **125,677,568 virtual decoded bytes**. Its `RDS5` table has 1,550 raw entries but **1,548 actual payload chunks**: entry 1548 is the terminal file/virtual boundary, and entry 1549 is zero padding (do not misclassify the terminal as another BC1 chunk). `ETPF` at `0x3100` has **912 texture descriptors**. Per the community-documented codec field, payload count is **1,402 zlib (0x20), 100 BCPack BC5 (0x14), 27 BC7 (0x1c), 18 BC1 (0x04), one BC3 (0x0c)**. All 1,402 zlib streams were actually inflated to their exact virtual lengths; all chunk offsets and zero padding validated. The 146 BCPack payloads are **not decoded/byte-verified yet**. **Max BCPack decoded chunk = 256 KiB** in this specific file; it does not cover large-buffer retention or BC4/BC6. Claude can create an **ignored local** byte-for-byte differential harness against current `bcpack::decode_into`, proposed rewrite and an independent decoder, with fuzz/malformed/8-byte feeder/256-history cases from synthetic or additional authorised files. Time BCPack-only vs zlib/end-to-end queues separately; this file is mostly zlib by decoded bytes (~109.3 MiB). Community layouts: [RDS5 reader](https://github.com/Ahmet-Dedeler/bcpack-rs/blob/master/src/ttpf.rs), [TTPF spec](https://github.com/BaptistSec/ttpf-spec/blob/main/SPEC.md), [VIctory decoder](../crates/victory-formats/src/bcpack.rs). **Gate remains** independent parity, Windows build and matched gameplay; do not check R30 complete yet.
- **R35 caution — disk payload vs RAM.** The TTPF's `~32.8 MiB` packed and `~119.9 MiB` virtual decoded sizes are **file properties, not resident process RAM, CPU heap retention or VRAM usage**. Per-worker scratch buffers added by [`2364a29`](https://github.com/Zaxix/VIctory/commit/2364a29a90710aeefb32d2a3677c72b85cdfcb57) must be measured by capacity/commit separately. Existing R35 baseline and subsystem-ownership gates remain open.
- [ ] **R41 — P2: Xbox FidelityFX loader/provider and RTX 4070 host-compatibility audit (new research candidate).** Owner supplied two **PE32+ Xbox DLLs**, `amd_fidelityfx_loader_xbox.dll` (13,312 B; SHA-256 `431bdd2e26c0c8214fd7601140eb9fe6d8a249f5000539453a9a9b6a0b7c0ee8`) and `amd_fidelityfx_upscaler_xbox.dll` (786,432 B; SHA-256 `faff02f294a7e9149ce079dffe3dedbcabe99b1f180ab14222aa8689e00a75fa`). Each exports `ffxConfigure`, `ffxCreateContext`, `ffxDestroyContext`, `ffxDispatch`, `ffxQuery`; the upscaler imports Xbox-specific `d3d12_xs.dll` and `xmem.dll`. Its bytes contain **29 structurally verified DXBC containers**, each with an `ACCC` section containing Xbox `FSPX` vendor payload, plus references to Xbox-specific `DeserializeComputePipelineStateX` and fallback `NewBE`. These are **not proved desktop DXIL or portable to NVIDIA**. Both DLLs lack PE certificate-table entries; authenticity not evaluated. The loader names an additional Xbox frame-generation DLL not supplied, which **does not prove FG use**. The current [`d3d12x.rs`](../crates/victory-hle/src/modules/d3d12x.rs) already handles `D3D12CreateRootSignatureDeserializer` for the AMD upscaler, while [`modules/mod.rs`](../crates/victory-hle/src/modules/mod.rs) lists no separate FidelityFX HLE module; the title loader may still map title DLLs, so audit actual imports/calls and HRESULTs before judging support. Public [GDK FSR sample](https://github.com/microsoft/Xbox-GDK-Samples/blob/main/Samples/Graphics/AMD_FSR/readme.md) separates PC `_dx12.dll` from Xbox `_xbox.dll` and describes optional Full/Lite exports **absent from these uploaded files**; no inference of their precise provider version, compatibility or missing feature from the mismatch alone. **Gate:** actual title-side FFX calls, provider selection, Xbox GPU interface coverage, supported PC fallback, correct motion vectors/depth/jitter, picture parity, latency, memory cost and measured RTX 4070 A/B. Avoid using the Xbox provider as a drop-in PC DLL or inventing Nvidia FSR4 support.
- **RPF9 archive and resource-file baseline — P3/reference only.** Owner's `common.rpf` is 6,642,192 B, `RPF9` magic and a **418-entry encrypted/high-entropy TOC**; header `w04=0x9580`, `w08=0x8520`, `w0c=0x3a10` agrees with the independent [RPF9 header geometry](https://github.com/JoshRob297/rpf9-research/blob/main/docs/RPF9.md), including a 10-byte/entry padded region. Static zlib streams include XML `rage__MediaDecoderSettings` with **Orbis (PlayStation)** budget field names, not valid Xbox/4070 memory limits. `resources.pri` is a 1,152 B Windows `mrm_pri2` app resource index naming splash/logo images; neither is a measured renderer bottleneck. Eight provided `msvcp140_*`, `concrt140`, `vcomp140`, `vccorlib140`, `vcruntime140` DLLs are MSVC support binaries; no custom optimisation justified absent loader/import failures or profiling. Do not assume PE certificate-table presence means a verified signature, or that missing uploaded dependencies are absent on the owner's machine.

## P0/P1 — First-world watchdog, cold compute and R33 prebuild research (2026-11-27)

**Evidence ownership:** The current [Zaxix/Defeat](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat) **IMPLEMENTATION STATUS — Claude-owned** table above is authoritative for what was implemented, tested and run on the 5800X/RTX 4070; research below does not update its states. New owner uploads: `game_scarlett_master.exe` (120,181,760 B), `shaders.rpf` (411,695,600 B), `appdata0.rpf` (8,880 B), `script.rpf` (4,358,960 B), `textures.rpf` (192,585,024 B), `textures_loose.ttpf` (21,081,856 B). Those six sizes are from **attachment metadata only** in this investigation: direct binary reading/disassembly was unavailable, so **none of their precise internal bytes, hashes, archive records or shader counts have been independently verified here**. The watchdog interpretation below is from published **independent 1.0.2.1 disassemblies**, not a claim of decompiling the uploaded EXE. No game code/assets committed.

- [ ] **R42 — P0: Understand the 1.0.2.1 title hang watchdog before changing any bypass.** Independent static investigations of the same build identify a thread starting at `exe+0x2215a60` / loop `+0x2215af6`: it sleeps **1,000 ms per poll** and counts **30 consecutive polls** without a change in the main-thread **QPC heartbeat** at `exe+0x71b6338`, written by `+0x22159f0`. The threshold is an **immediate `0x1e`**, *not* a 30 FPS threshold, GPU execution-duration limit or adjustable title setting. Reported heartbeat writer call sites are `+0x120799`, `+0xc9e368`, `+0x1c82b67`, `+0x1c82bc1`; the latter two occur inside a wait/pump loop, so some waits keep the watchdog satisfied. A **different** path checks 64 in-flight scope timestamps at `+0x6844a20` (0x48 stride), gated by a scope counter `+0x71b6334`. Identify which path fired using the counter, callback and last heartbeat writer stack, not the crash offset alone. A callback at `+0x23cf620` constructs the label **"GPU Loc Sig"**, its deliberate abort is `+0x23cf683` (11-byte `mov dword ptr [0],0x4d8bd421`), with a fallback `+0x2215c35`; the label does **not** prove a GPU stall. [XCL #505 disassembly and field locations](https://github.com/xcl-dev/xcl/issues/505), [XCL #1164 independent heartbeat/QPC confirmation](https://github.com/xcl-dev/xcl/issues/1164), [VIctory PR #454 guarded opt-in watchdog hook](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/454). **Exact observational definition:** 30 failed one-second progress polls (approximately 30 s wall time, scheduler variation); no claim this particular uploaded EXE matches the public BLAKE3/build until fingerprint and byte offsets checked. **Gate:** collect version/hash and disassembly, heartbeat timestamp/writer-thread ID, frames/PSO wait stacks, queue in-flight and last submit before abort. Avoid treating `VICTORY_NO_WATCHDOG` as a fix: upstream #454 is a build/byte-guarded *diagnostic bypass*, off by default, and genuine deadlocks remain.
- **R42 / current main implementation nuance (P0):** [`pipeline/make.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline/make.rs#L192-L320) now has `VICTORY_PIPELINE_COMPUTE_WAIT_MS=10000` (0 = unlimited), introduced after the owner's first watchdog crash; on run 2 this was reached on nine frames. Code checks elapsed compute time **before** calling `p.make()` and charges it **after return**. Therefore this is an **admission budget, not a hard time limit**: one driver compilation or wait on another worker's `OnceLock` can last beyond the remaining budget, and several title recording threads can pass the initial test before accounting. This is a **source-level risk, not a proven new deadlock**. Audit per-call long tails and time between QPC heartbeats before changing the logic; don't blindly drop one-shot compute passes, which can leave textures/windows wrong for an entire scene. [fxceless one-shot compute rationale](https://github.com/fxceless/victory-fork/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline/make.rs#L189-L244), [independent XCL compute-compile stalls](https://github.com/xcl-dev/xcl/issues/505). 
- [ ] **R33 expanded — P1: Pack-aware host PSO prebuild is missing in Defeat, shader warmup alone is not equivalent.** The current [`main.rs`](../apps/victory/src/main.rs#L70-L94) uses `translate --warm <hash-file>` to fill **shader translations**, while [`translate.rs`](../apps/victory/src/translate.rs) only creates optional **per-shader diagnostic host pipelines** with `--pipelines`; it does not reconstruct the title's actual composite PSOs. Defeat lacks `apps/victory/src/prebuild.rs`, `victory-game/src/shaderpack.rs`, and `victory-game/src/pipelines.rs` (checked repository paths), so porting the upstream prebuild is **not just CLI wiring**. [Upstream `translate --cache --pipelines`](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/blob/main/apps/victory/src/main.rs), [`prebuild.rs`](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/blob/main/apps/victory/src/prebuild.rs), [`shaderpack.rs`](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-game/src/shaderpack.rs), [`pipelines.rs`](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-game/src/pipelines.rs), [`sgd5.rs`](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-game/src/sgd5.rs), [`pipeline.rs::Prebuild`](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline.rs#L606-L705). Upstream finds `PS\\0\\x09` shader-pack records (including concatenated 16-byte-aligned zlib streams in an archive), identifies programs by their first two native VOP3 `v_nop` hash instructions, joins SGD5 shader containers by the 24-byte ID, reconstructs title graphics state and compute PSOs through **runtime-equivalent host code**, and writes them to the D3D12 host pipeline library. Each skipped/unnamed pipeline reports a reason; the prebuild loads/skips existing entries, prioritizes earlier-used PSOs, and periodically saves for resume.
- **R33 diagnostic opportunity — enumerate runtime-equivalent prebuild keys without compiling PSOs (source inspection, 2026-11-27):** Upstream [`apps/victory/src/prebuild.rs`](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/blob/main/apps/victory/src/prebuild.rs) implements `VICTORY_PREBUILD_KEYS=<output-file>`. It parses the supplied pack and shader containers, calls the same `victory_gpu::pipeline::Prebuild::key()` / `make::key()` logic as upstream runtime, writes one tab-separated key, pack record and shader/state description for reconstructable entries, and a `-` plus concrete skip reason for unreconstructable entries. It also counts depth-stencil/rasterizer/blend state provenance; in this diagnostic path it does **not compile** the host PSOs. This is **upstream only**, not an existing Defeat flag; Defeat's three pack/prebuild modules are still absent. **Practical research gate:** on authorized locally available shader data, enumerate and sort the keys, tally compute vs graphics and skip categories, then compare with the owner's actual startup `VICTORY_PIPELINE_LOG`/early-list keys and missing-PSO diagnostics. Upstream's key hashes the shader stage and content hash plus `GraphicsState::describe()`; Defeat's [`Pending::new`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline/make.rs) has the same apparent hash inputs, but **description equivalence, translated shader identity, host options and persistent library compatibility are not established** merely by matching the hash formula. Prioritize actual first-world keys and one-shot compute stages rather than assuming every pack entry is required, and treat unmatched/unnamed states as coverage gaps, never guessed PSOs. This is a source-backed diagnostic path, **not** a new gameplay measurement or a port.
- **R33 prebuild correctness gates:** Use on-owner RTX 4070 with **same driver, adapter, host-wave mode, view-limit, translator hash, shader toggles, root signature and render target formats** as `run`; do not reuse a blob with incompatible fingerprints or count merely translated shaders as fully built host PSOs. Match runtime *pipeline keys and state byte-for-byte*. Distinguish shader-cache misses, driver PSO creation, library loads/refusals, PSOs skipped because state not named, missing/failed compute stages, dynamic target-format variants and remaining dropped draws. Compare A=clean cold, B=shader-warm only, C=full PSO prebuilt; capture prebuild offline wall time/disk bytes, loading/world entry, `pipeline made` vs `loaded`, PSO queue peak, refused/dropped draws, image parity and watchdog status. A [PR #189 branch report](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/2602) claims early-only prebuild cleared the in-progress PSO queue but still had **2,534 drops due to unsupported shaders** and massive GPU frame time; it is a **contributor, mismatched-hardware report**, not current upstream-main or 4070 performance. Its `VICTORY_PREBUILD_EARLY_ONLY` flag is **not present in the examined upstream main source**; do not document it as a current main feature without verifying.
- **R33 / startup workload and fairness:** [Upstream `pipeline/make.rs`](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline/make.rs) has background workers and a nominal 500 ms *per-title-frame* pipeline wait; [fxceless](https://github.com/fxceless/victory-fork/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline/make.rs#L312-L370) shares that budget across title recording threads and uses default unbounded one-shot compute waits to prevent losing vital first-use dispatches. Without shared accounting, five threads once each waited 500 ms = **~2.5 s total** in a frame (source comment). Defeat inherited shared budget and now has the 10 s compute admission cap. Its **actual run 2** still saw ~540–690 PSOs queued, 258–3398 dropped draws per frame, 3 driver `E_INVALIDARG` compute refusals, CPU saturation, and small zlib requests up to 1.45 s; PSO prebuild may reduce contention but **cannot fix refused shaders, texture coherence or long GPU dispatches**. Measure `VICTORY_PIPELINE_WORKERS` and `VICTORY_DSTORAGE_WORKERS` under R31 using WPR ready time, not just counts.
- **Six uploaded files — prioritized research tasks (metadata only, not yet decoded):** `game_scarlett_master.exe` (the actual build fingerprint, WDG1/WDG2 call graph, QPC heartbeat, first-run import and PSO call sites); `shaders.rpf` (candidate SGD5 shader databases and `PS\\0\\x09` pack, file 393 MiB < upstream 2 GiB scan limit; confirm archive scanner finds/completes pack); `appdata0.rpf` (small startup-critical RPF9; independent project reproduced pre-window breakpoint when missing: [upstream README](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory)); `script.rpf` (RPF9 startup scripts/stream timing and resource references); `textures.rpf` (model/texture stream workloads, no direct inference of BCPack format from 16-byte block alignment: [RPF9 texture study](https://github.com/JoshRob297/rpf9-research/blob/main/docs/TEXTURES.md)); `textures_loose.ttpf` (RDS5/ETPF chunk codec census and BC1/3/4/5/6/7 validation against prior `ttdb_graphics.ttpf`, [RDS5 layout](https://github.com/Ahmet-Dedeler/bcpack-rs/blob/master/src/ttpf.rs)). Do not extract/distribute protected game assets; retain local-only per-chunk hashed tests. **No claims of additional codec counts, actual EXE BLAKE3 or shader-pack presence from this upload until bytes are inspected.**

### R14 binary research — owner-supplied XboxBvhBuilder.dll (read-only, 2026-11-27)

- **Verified attached DLL, not a gameplay result:** The owner's `XboxBvhBuilder - Copy.zip` contains one `884,224`-byte PE32+ x86-64 DLL, SHA-256 `99f803ac22c01ef329dddb1d40be67238d2a812d01e8d040b3cb5135bc23707e`. PE .xbld strings say `April 2025 GXDK Update 5` / `10.0.26100.4125`; native exports include `BuildRaytracingAccelerationStructureBatched2X` (RVA `0x216a0`), `GetRaytracingAccelerationStructurePrebuildInfo` (`0x21880`), `ValidateRehydrateAccelerationStructureBuildDesc` (`0x21a00`), and `Set64BitMortonCodes` (`0x21bd0`). Exact bytes checked locally from the supplied ZIP, not inferred from similarly sized public binaries.
- **New byte-level evidence:** 52 separately bounded and unique DXBC containers, each with 9 recognized parts including DXIL/RTS0/PSV0, all compute shader model 6.5; 16 distinct RTS0 payloads; PSV thread groups: 35 x 32, 7 x 1024, 4 x 64, 4 x 512, one 256 and one 1. The DLL's external build-batch array steps `0x50` B per record (internal normalized array can step `0x40`), postbuild count at +`0x30`, pointer at +`0x38`, records of `0x10` B, and `CopyRaytracingAccelerationStructure` export operates only when copy mode = 1 (COMPACT). The rehydration validator (RVA `0x21a00`) accumulates a CRC-style table checksum of the build inputs and compares it with source-header dword2 (+8), returning `0x80070057` on mismatch. The exact checksum polynomial/geometry-field coverage and complete shader programs have **not** been decompiled or runtime-validated.
- **Builder constraints and NVIDIA relevance:** User DLL strings impose 256-byte AS alignment, destination address < `2^40`, BVH size ≤2 GiB, consistent update flags/source and index/geometry layout. [XCL 40-bit address issue](https://github.com/ivall/xcl/issues/585) independently observed the same exception; [five-star-recomp BVH layout analysis](https://github.com/ThatRecompDev/five-star-recomp/blob/main/docs/raytracing.md) has a 0x50-byte batch, header CRC/root and CPU BLAS/TLAS reference; [XCL's console-list experiments](https://github.com/xcl-dev/xcl/blob/main/HANDOFF.md) validate actual DLL-produced BVHs on another GPU; [fxceless](https://github.com/fxceless/victory-fork/blob/main/README.md) reports severe title-memory synchronization cost with builder on (different PC). These are separate evidence categories, not Defeat RTX 4070 benchmarks. Group size 64/1024 **does not prove** a WaveSize(64) requirement: [NVIDIA wave64 PSO refusals](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/2220) are a candidate to test against the owner's **three** `E_INVALIDARG` compute PSOs, not a proven match. A desktop DXR BVH is not byte-compatible with the console node representation; [XCL's host DXR mirror proposal](https://github.com/xcl-dev/xcl/issues/1751) is not an implemented fix.
- **Do not duplicate Claude's new code:** [`c8bdf90`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/commit/c8bdf9094a159bcc1652d04d305628e4a03fcf9f) now queues compute PSOs to workers at creation and records dropped-draw reasons (see Claude's run 4 in the implementation section). Existing `pipeline.rs` already gates builder all-or-none and has opt-in `VICTORY_BVH_BUILDER=1`; run 4 had builder **off**, so the three refused PSOs cannot yet be assigned to this DLL. Next: use actual log hashes, debugger/error reason, 52-kernel inventory and serialized 0x40-byte header/CRC fixture to correlate stages and one-shot dispatches. **Gate remains open:** first correctly rendered world, all essential stages, output parity and long-run NVIDIA memory/coherence correctness. Full owner-facing local research TXT: `XboxBvhBuilder_Reverse_Engineering_Research_2026-11-27.txt` (not committed; no game binary or extracted shader bytes committed).

### R43 — Current-feature optimization audit: effective defaults and RTX 4070 critical path (research-only, 2026-11-27)

- [ ] **R43 / P0: reconcile code-effective configuration with actual launch environment.** Checked `main` after Claude's run-4 changes (`c7646c5`). Some nearby source comments and historic checklist entries describe *older defaults*, not what current code returns: [`resource.rs::room_percent`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/resource.rs) defaults to **96%** (clamped 50–97), although the comment says 90%; [`targets_room_kept`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/resource.rs) defaults to **2000 MiB**, although its header comment says 3072 MiB; [`shadow.rs::window_card_most`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) defaults to **1280 MiB** although its header describes 0; [`window_upload`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) defaults **on** unless `VICTORY_WINDOW_UPLOAD=0`, although the preceding comment says off. Other verified branches: `VICTORY_CARD_ROOM_FOR_BUFFERS` on, `VICTORY_HEAP_WORKER` off unless 1, `VICTORY_LEVELS_ON_DEMAND` off unless 1 plus tiled tier >=2, `VICTORY_HEAP_PASS_MARKED` off unless 1/alt, `VICTORY_TEXTURE_CATCHUP` on (3x when >=1024 due slots), `VICTORY_TEXTURE_STAGE_MB` 192 and `VICTORY_TEXTURE_STAGE_MS` 250 before catchup. The conditional `VICTORY_TILES_SMALL_WHEN_FULL_MB` default is **256 MiB** when levels-on-demand is in use, despite an older 0-default comment. Actual environment variables, device support, current branch SHA and runtime log must be recorded: **do not infer which paths ran merely from code defaults.** No source changes by research assistant.
- [ ] **R43 / P0: classify why run 4 has ~24,700 indexed texture slots queued and 75–310 ms heap passes.** In [`draw.rs::State::heap_textures`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs), due slots may be missing/unmapped, partially mapped, waiting for VRAM, previously-uploaded-but-stale or a different view; the count is **slots, not necessarily unique visible textures**. `VICTORY_FULL_CARD_PASS` and `VICTORY_REUPLOAD_WHEN_FULL` are already on by default; `resource::hosted` already retries transient `NoTexture::ForNow`. Read `HEAP_TEXTURES_QUEUE` census, `card accounting` split, indexed-view nulls/held reasons, actual mapped bytes and work microseconds before proposing admission logic. At full card, a pass can still examine up to 8192 due slots and defer many; `VICTORY_TEXTURE_CATCHUP` raises workload allowance, not available VRAM. The opt-in [`heap_pass.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/heap_pass.rs) marked-slot algorithm applies to the **buffer-view** heap pass, **not automatically** to this texture queue: do not cite it as a measured texture-queue fix. Gate: demonstrate reduced wait+heap-pass time, fewer black/incorrect textures, no stale descriptor or one-shot upload loss.
- [ ] **R43 / P1: controlled, one-variable-at-a-time study of *existing* knobs before writing a replacement manager:** record `VICTORY_TEXTURE_ROOM_PERCENT` (e.g. baseline 96 vs 94/92), `VICTORY_CARD_ROOM_FOR_TARGETS_MB` (baseline 2000 vs 2400/3072), `VICTORY_HEAP_WORKER=1` (off baseline), `VICTORY_LEVELS_ON_DEMAND=1` (off baseline, hardware tier and zero/black mip validation), and only if level-by-level is validated `VICTORY_TILES_SMALL_WHEN_FULL_MB` (actual conditional baseline 256). Separate `VICTORY_CARD_BUFFERS_MB`/window policies, one-shot compute, driver PSO errors, and `VICTORY_DSTORAGE_WORKERS` × `VICTORY_PIPELINE_WORKERS` tests. Avoid `VICTORY_TEXTURE_BUDGET_MB` as a quick fix: the absolute override bypasses the dynamic DXGI-budget guard. Compare identical render settings/caches/scene, account for the DXGI budget itself changing, log CPU prepare, GPU busy, heap pass, copy-back, missing draws, VRAM local/nonlocal, resident texture/tile bytes, visual screenshots, and 30–60 min DRED. No predicted FPS gain.
- [ ] **R43 / P0/P1: isolate run-4 refusals from the BVH-builder question.** Claude recorded three `E_INVALIDARG` compute PSOs with `VICTORY_BVH_BUILDER=0`. [fxceless issue #923](https://github.com/fxceless/victory-fork/issues/923) reports *different hardware/build* compute refusals in particle-emitter stages; [upstream #2220](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/2220) separately shows WaveSize(64)-vs-NVIDIA-wave32 refusals. Match Defeat's exact shader hashes and D3D12 debug-layer HRESULTs before attributing them to wave64, particle programs or XboxBvhBuilder. A shader/PSO refusal is not cured by more memory or offline prebuild; a missing textured draw is not necessarily missing ray-traced illumination. Maintain explicit `pipeline making/refused/not bound/builder off` reasons.
- **Sources and gates:** [Defeat run-4 implementation status](RESEARCH_CHECKLIST.md), [D3D12 budget/usage](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/dxgi1_4/ns-dxgi1_4-dxgi_query_video_memory_info), [GPU_UPLOAD heaps](https://microsoft.github.io/DirectX-Specs/d3d/D3D12GPUUploadHeaps.html), [PIX GPU memory](https://devblogs.microsoft.com/pix/gpu-memory-usage/), [WPA ready-time](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/test/wpt/cpu-analysis). Research handoff saved locally as `Defeat_Existing_Features_Optimization_Research_2026-11-27.txt`; no performance measurements conducted here.

### R44 — Phase-two source audit: further optimization leads and measurement gates (research-only, 2026-11-27)

- [ ] **R44 / P0: budget/admission accuracy before inventing a new allocator.** [`resource::room`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/resource.rs) grants a *boolean* admission based on current DXGI usage, held target/buffer reserves, and its texture-room fraction, not the bytes of the candidate texture. [`room_lately`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/resource.rs) caches that yes/no answer for up to 100 ms. [`State::heap_textures`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs) revisits its admission answer at intervals of work, but allocations can exceed the residual headroom; this is an **unverified contributor** to run-4 VRAM saturation, not a proven cause. Measure requested/allocated bytes, between-check allocations, budget changes, queued *unique visible resources* versus slots and the reason for refusal. Proposed future per-class reservation must include committed cost, in-flight work and transient doubles; do not bypass DXGI's dynamic budget with `VICTORY_TEXTURE_BUDGET_MB`.
- [ ] **R44 / P1: test dormant memory-watch fast paths after establishing a correct baseline.** [`watch.rs::absent_once`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/watch.rs) (off by default, `VICTORY_ABSENT_ONCE=alt|1`, normal `VICTORY_ABSENT_MS=100`) caches negative 64 KiB mapping results to reduce repeated kernel page-table walks; [`watch.rs::light`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/watch.rs) (off, `VICTORY_LIGHT_REFRESH=alt|1`) can answer refresh requests from published clean-page bits without guards lock/kernel call. Their benefit is **not established on this RTX 4070**. Compare identical scenes with kernel-call counts, guard-wait milliseconds, stale-copy detection, streaming arrival and write-watch coherence. `VICTORY_ABSENT_MS=told` depends on *complete* title-memory remap/commit notifications and should not be the first experiment.
- [ ] **R44 / P1/P2: avoid redundant submission work and GPU copy/hash ping-pong.** [`host.rs::sync_batched`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) implements `VICTORY_CARD_SYNC_BATCH=alt|1` (off by default): records copies, one grouped transition and hashes, instead of toggling copy and compute per card buffer. Its dramatic source-comment timings came from a different machine/workload; measure GPU card lists and CPU submit cost on Defeat, especially if many small card-buffer copies are admitted. [`queue.rs::join_parts`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/queue.rs) (`VICTORY_JOIN_PARTS=alt|1`, off) coalesces eligible wait-separated segments; [`queue.rs::behind`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/queue.rs) (`VICTORY_ASYNC_SUBMIT=1`, off) moves preparation to a worker but changes sequencing/visibility. Test each separately with fence-order checks, no stale readback and exact screenshots. `VICTORY_GPU_TIMING` **disables** the late-flight path via `host.rs::late_stage`: never compare its instrumented FPS to an uninstrumented baseline as if mode were unchanged.
- [ ] **R44 / P1: audit DirectStorage staging head-of-line blocking and CPU-worker contention.** [`dstorage.rs::Lanes::start`](../crates/victory-hle/src/modules/dstorage.rs) selects the most deserving priority class/lane **before** checking if that request fits the 32 MiB staging budget and returns `None` when it cannot fit; other runnable requests may remain unexamined until a completion (a possible priority-independent convoy, **hypothesis**). First build a deterministic synthetic scheduler fixture (priority fairness, statuses, page destinations and zero-starvation), then compare ready-time and staging-wait counters. On the 8C/16T Ryzen 5800X, the default 16 DirectStorage and 12 pipeline workers can compete with game+submission+texture threads; profile WPR ready and running time before tuning the `VICTORY_DSTORAGE_WORKERS` × `VICTORY_PIPELINE_WORKERS` matrix. Existing R30 decoder arithmetic rewrite was tried and rejected; don't redo it.
- [ ] **R44 / P2: scope CPU/GPU copy-back by observed reads, never assume stores are dead.** [`shadow.rs::copy_back_with`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) merges differences at 64 KiB granularity and preserves title writes under its snapshot/guard rules. [`host.rs::hand_back`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) coalesces declared ranges; instrument `VICTORY_BACK_READS` first. [fxceless report](https://github.com/fxceless/victory-fork/blob/main/README.md) independently sampled a large share of GPU copy-backs without later CPU reads; that is *other hardware/scene evidence*, not permission to defer these writes on the owner's game. A future lazy scheme requires read-fault integration, page remap/generation, coherent atomic ordering, GPU lifetime, aliasing and title synchronization proofs; fail closed on unknown consumers.
- **Research artifact (not committed to repository):** `Defeat_Performance_Research_Continuation_2026-11-27.txt`, paired with local read-only `Defeat_Log_Triage_2026-11-27.py` (groups matching log text into CSV; it does not prove causes). Continuation is additive to R43, does not supply new runtime performance figures and does not change application code. Vendor reference: [DXGI memory budgets](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/dxgi1_4/ns-dxgi1_4-dxgi_query_video_memory_info), [PIX critical path](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/pix/timing-captures/critical-path-analysis), [D3D12 GBV overhead](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/direct3d12/using-d3d12-debug-layer-gpu-based-validation), [WPA CPU analysis](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/test/wpt/cpu-analysis).

### R45 — Kimi memory-coherency report: verified integration and critical corrections (research-only, 2026-11-27)

- [ ] **R45 / P0: establish the *actual* normal submission mode before any memory model changes.** The 1,208-line Kimi study correctly maps many subsystems but incorrectly calls unset `VICTORY_LATE_WAIT` fully synchronous: [`shadow.rs::relay`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) is on by default in ordinary non-test runs and [`host.rs::late_stage`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) then chooses stage 3; `VICTORY_GPU_TIMING` **present at any value** forces stage 0. Environment overrides / `VICTORY_KEEP_SNAPSHOTS` alter this. Separate normal-game FPS from GPU-timing diagnostic runs; never attribute their difference to candidate optimization alone.
- [ ] **R45 / P0: do NOT implement Kimi's lazy-readback PAGE_READONLY/VEH pseudocode.** [`watch.rs::protect_here`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/watch.rs) uses `PAGE_READONLY` specifically to intercept WRITES; CPU READS are permitted. [Windows protection reference](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/memory/memory-protection-constants): read interception needs a different page-protection/fault model (`PAGE_NOACCESS` or one-shot `PAGE_GUARD`), all-alias handling, remap generation, guest CPU service-reader coverage and a proven handler/submit/relay lock-order graph. [`backreads.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/backreads.rs) is a **passive** 1-in-8 sampled working-set touch probe (`VICTORY_BACK_READS=2`), NOT a read-before-access interception primitive or correctness proof. Keep lazy merge opt-in research, unknown consumer -> eager.
- [ ] **R45 / P0: measure the owner's 120–420ms 'copy-back' BEFORE diagnosing CPU memcmp.** Kimi inferred 3–10GB/frame repeated comparison from overlapping views, but [`host.rs::hand_back`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) already union-coalesces declared spans per shadow and [`shadow.rs::copy_back_with`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) differs PC shadows from GPU-local card copies with GPU-side compare/fetch. Log true per-placement compared bytes and `BackScanUs`/`BackSyncUs`, GPU card hash/fetch/wait, relay CPU wait and p95 frame critical path. No attribution or predicted FPS gain until E1 measurement.
- [ ] **R45 / P1: investigate two *open upstream* branches without conflating their results or porting wholesale.** [VIctory PR #3102](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/3102): 5070 Ti **synthetic** 200-dispatch real-path GPU-local buffer placement, e.g. 58.1→29.0ms; author states **no NVIDIA in-title test** and reports AMD title regression. Defeat already has 1MiB/1024MiB GPU-local card buffers and GPU_UPLOAD windows, but not PR's full broader RO-buffer GPU_UPLOAD / writable-VRAM+CPU-mirror / budgets/UMA/residency stack. [PR #2958](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/2958): 4070 SUPER **in-title** 411–414→281–283ms with wave-coalesced *existing write marks*, but Defeat main does not have that upstream per-view mark-emission implementation. [PR #2465](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/2465) places per-view marks in VRAM and saw **no clear in-title gain** on RTX 5090; [PR #2175](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/2175) applies to 40-bit raw title-memory page marks. These mechanisms overlap: test system-memory coalescing vs VRAM+submission readback/clear vs combination, not additive assumed wins; record staging/VRAM-budget cost and exact shader coverage.
- [ ] **R45 / correctness: audit raw-address and guest-memory lifetime before suppressing write-back.** [`memory.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/memory.rs) says a raw shader address absent from its page directory yields read 0 / dropped GPU store until next frame, and every participating dispatch may declare/refresh all numbered copies; separate **builder-on** stress from owner's **builder-off** run 4. [`gpu_access.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/gpu_access.rs) flags are telemetry-only due to a `VirtualFree` blind spot; same-dword CPU/GPU atomics remain R13 open. No read-lazy scheme without guest fence semantics, remap alias/generation, all observer interception, queue-fence retirement, fault-recursion and deterministic race tests. At/over DXGI budget (run4), additional GPU-local copies may worsen visible texture starvation.
- **Full reviewed handoff (local generated artifacts, no code edits):** `Defeat_Memory_Coherency_Integrated_Review_2026-11-27.txt` (19 corrections, actual source paths, upstream validation, experiment matrix); `Defeat_Performance_Master_Integrated_Research_2026-11-27.txt` (the reviewed handoff plus **the entire original Kimi 1,208-line text** and prior R43/R44 studies unchanged). Vendor crosschecks: [Windows PAGE_GUARD](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/Memory/creating-guard-pages), [GPU_UPLOAD specification](https://microsoft.github.io/DirectX-Specs/d3d/D3D12GPUUploadHeaps.html), [NVIDIA GPU_UPLOAD guidance](https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/optimizing-dx12-resource-uploads-to-the-gpu-using-gpu-upload-heaps/). **Research-only**; Claude retains application-code and implementation-status authority.

### R46 — Hydrogen VRAM report: independently audited source claims and safe next-test gates (research-only, 2026-11-27)

- [ ] **R46 / P0: correct the module attribution before modifying anything.** Hydrogen's report claims [`heap_pass.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/heap_pass.rs) drives the ~24.7k indexed **texture** backlog; source says it implements the **buffer-view** heap pass (opt-in `VICTORY_HEAP_PASS_MARKED`). The texture queue lives in [`draw.rs::State::heap_textures`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs) + [`descriptor/host.rs::TextureQueue`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/descriptor/host.rs); the last census is queued/due/unmapped/partly/oldest, **not unique missing textures**. `texture_slots_due(frame, 8192)` is a candidate cap, not 8192 allocations. Existing unmapped slot retry is already exponential (1/2/4/... up to 8–64 frames), with remap, recheck, room-return and full-card rescheduling. Do NOT implement generic permanent null-slot backoff from Hydrogen or assume its proposed <5ms target.
- [ ] **R46 / P0: investigate exact [VIctory PR #3198](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/3198), NOT the report's invented broader retry system.** The open PR fixes a cached *hostless placed texture* left in `PLACED_TEXTURES` after host creation failure; it does **not** add `retry_count`, exponential backoff or `heap_pass.rs` rework. [`resource.rs::texture_at`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/resource.rs) caches guest resources and `hosted` distinguishes `NoTexture::Refused` and `ForNow` with its own temporary no-room retry and `descriptor::texture_made` notification. Trace the precise reproduction and write regression tests before porting any fix; cached hostless resources are not automatically the cause of queued slots. Placed texture key already normalizes unused desc words 1/13.
- [ ] **R46 / P0: dry-run and measure size-aware GPU admission rather than claiming proven WDDM thrash.** [`resource.rs::room`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/resource.rs) is Boolean/current-usage based (2GiB floor, default 96% DXGI budget share, target reserve 2000MiB, card-buffer reserve) and **not candidate-size aware**. Yet [`draw.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs) already rechecks `room()` after 32MiB staged; `room_lately` caches separately for 100ms. Track actual `GetResourceAllocationInfo` estimates, live host allocations, unique backing, twin/tile heap bytes, budget age and per-attempt failures. A reservation ledger must avoid double-counting already reported `CurrentUsage` and handle async commits/failures/releases. No fixed 'safe 9.8GB', no 512MB universal margin, and no fps prediction without owner A/B.

[`shadow.rs::card_limits`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) already defaults to 1MiB/1024MiB, the [Belobug #444](https://github.com/Belobug/VIctory-Belobug-AMD/pull/444) tier. Default local-wave approximation may produce **valid host shaders with incorrect guest results**: offline classify actual hashes and validate semantic parity for cross-half ballots/lane reads/LDS and diverging branches before gameplay opt-ins.

[`pipeline/make.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline/make.rs) now schedules compute PSOs early by default after Claude's c8bdf90, with a default **10s total per-frame compute wait** and default 500ms graphic wait. Confirm it in owner's next baseline and preserve one-shot compute correctness: a dropped dispatch may leave a broken scene until restart. `VICTORY_GPU_TIMING` disables late scheduling, so don't compare its FPS to a normal run.

[Open PR #189](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/189) provides alternate exact/safe-stop Wave64 modes, with reported 97→0 driver PSO refusals BUT 26 additional translator refusals in unsafe cases; [open PR #1845](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/1845) bundles five NVIDIA changes and claims 113→0 on RTX5080. Compare opcode-level behavior, not headline numbers, and preserve precise shader-cache keys / GPU tests.

[`host.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) already supports `VICTORY_D3D12_BACKGROUND=0`, but driver background shader work may affect final GPU code quality; compare separately with exact warmed caches, identical screenshots and no unsupported FPS predictions.

No builder-on global blanket until all builder stages and 40-bit memory copy writes are correct, and no `VICTORY_GROUP_SYNC=0` accepted as a correctness fix.

[victory-perf merged #22](https://github.com/vitutu1/victory-perf/pull/22) adds 8-dword ResourceHeap image GetDimensions fallback, but [Defeat `unused_descriptor_kind`](../crates/victory-shader/src/emit/lower.rs) **already** reconstructs selectively 8-dword images and 4-dword buffer record counts; five Run9 failures were different dynamic `s_load_dwordx4` cases. ### R55 — Run11 owner evidence + Claude's six optimization research questions: correctness gates and scoped PRs (research-only, 2026-11-27)

> **Owner-supplied Run11 evidence, not a controlled A/B:** `run11.zip`/`run11.txt` (25,647 lines, 13:32:34–13:40:14 UTC) and `gpu11.csv` (whole-GPU counters). Research source pin: `main` `2618506016015194f5eef07d68c1f7aceca4ee3e` before this note. Claude retains code, implementation-status, tests and game runs. This section adds *research only*, never marks implementation done or changes existing Run10/R54 notes.

- [ ] **R55 / P0 Run11 critical path, source-bounded:** 48 >300-draw frame-reporting windows: median **195.2ms/frame** (p90 632.55ms), median **111.9ms preparing** and 78.95ms copying back, versus 21.9ms GPU (different components may overlap). 254 verbose preparing reports over that world-time envelope: median **31.6ms write-watch/in-flight checks**, **36.15ms per-list steps**, **8.7ms flush refreshes**, with p90 77.9/92.4/28.5ms. These are sampling/report windows, not strictly synchronized per-frame attribution: first instrument same-submission CPU critical path, watch/guard lock waits, list-step counts, card-before GPU+CPU timing and first-use PSO before assuming an additive gain. Run11 FAST reports ON; no logged device removal.
- [ ] **R55 / P0 Run11 sparse mip gate remains active; separate theoretical bytes from DXGI:** `VICTORY_LEVELS_ON_DEMAND=1` final line: **1,412 tiled textures alive, 229MB mapped tile bytes, 3,035MB not allocated of 3,265MB full theoretical, 576MB of tile-pool heaps**; cumulative **9,173 UpdateTileMappings**, **1,142 uploads lacking a held mip**, **1,082 reuploads**, and **0 currently all-black tiled views**. This does **not** prove a net 3,035MB DXGI/local VRAM saving or image parity: `tiles.rs` keeps tiles to host-copy drop; the pool retains heaps; `VICTORY_TILES_SMALL_WHEN_FULL_MB` now defaults **256**, despite older 'off' comment. Keep FAST and builder-off fixed, compare matched save/image, tile allocation/heap free, LOD, view remaking, p50/p95 and truly simultaneous LOCAL budget/usage; only then accept the feature.
- [ ] **R55 / P0 code-default correction — `STEP_INDEX` and `FILLS_PLAIN` are already on:** current `host.rs::step_index` and `fills_plain` both return **1 for unset** (`_ => 1`), contrary to their old 'off by default' comments and old README. `VICTORY_STEP_INDEX=0` or `VICTORY_FILLS_PLAIN=0` is the actual off comparison; `FILLS_PLAIN` also requires `COPY_PLAIN` (FAST ON). `grouped` preserves first-seen shadow/range order with a randomized parity unit test; **but** `stale_under_flights` snapshots `writes_pending()` once per shadow versus per-step old checks, so test concurrent relay completion under ASYNC_SUBMIT/QUEUE2. `copy_fill` replaces each under-256-byte kernel copy by guarded `rep movsb`, falling back on fault: no intentional refresh skip, but test remap/fault/partial destination and title writes. Do not claim wins from merely setting both to 1 on Run11.
- [ ] **R55 / P1 `LIGHT_REFRESH` conditional exactness and existing real-game measurements:** `watch.rs::light_mode` defaults **0**. It skips GUARDS/`GetWriteWatch` only where all 64KiB `PUBLISHED` bits are clean and journal versions say no writes; requires tracked alias/guard faults, `title_memory_written`, DirectStorage PageWindow and remap notices, own writes and fence ordering. A hole filled without notification can remain stale until the bounded hole scan detects it; upstream [#3481](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/3481) demonstrates a late-mapped view missed before PageWindow drop. Existing base **[diagnostics run294](../diagnostics/README.md)** already measured `LIGHT_REFRESH=alt`: ~92% view asks answered by bits, **preparing -10.8ms** and copies -13.7ms at equal draws, **but frame +7ms ±5** and no clear wall/FPS gain; left off. **Run297** already toggled `FILLS_PLAIN`+`STEP_INDEX` with two other switches: preparing -8.9ms ±4.7, frame +11ms ±11.5, not individual gains. Correctness test with aliases+mid-write/remap+ASYNC before opting in; do not treat these as untested switches.

Require page-set byte-for-byte, 4KiB/64KiB, alias/remap/DirectStorage tests before source edits.

Every GPU-write mark needs a complete writer/alias census, fence-safe clear/readback and per-page golden comparison. **[#3089](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/3089) is an issue**, conditional withheld-page mark optimization; not evidence all GPU or CPU writes are marked. The AMD [Belobug renderer port](https://github.com/Belobug/VIctory-Belobug-AMD/tree/a-publier) journal/descriptor patches are title/build-specific and not safe blind ports.

GPU marks already exist for descriptor views/raw title memory. First count which **1,500 pieces are GPU hashes** versus actual CPU title-memory re-reads, waits/fetches, and source CPU dirty pages. Neither a shader-side bit nor a GPU hash detects a CPU modification not yet observed/uploaded. No speculative skip of compare/copyback absent exhaustive store-instrumentation coverage, full byte-level parity and fencing.

They collectively report **4,370 made PSOs**, **4,638 shader-cache hits**, **59 fresh shader translations** (different counting units); slow samples are mostly host D3D12 PSO/library/driver time: 13:37:04 10 PSOs avg **833ms**, **5% translation**; 13:36:00 11 avg **297ms**, **7%**; 13:38:45 2 avg **393ms**, **1%**. Warning: a pathological 15334% translation reporting window has unrelated denominator, do not use. Code's first-use graphics wait is **500ms/frame**, and `pipeline/make.rs` already prewarms used-key PSOs + up to two spare compute workers. Record shader ID, key, exact queue/driver/library/translate timings, first-use frame and dropped one-shot dispatches before opting into bounded additional PSO prebuild; do not claim 10–19s solely driver stalls.

Use `VICTORY_SHADER_FRESH=<five> VICTORY_DESC_WHY=1` **offline only**, dump four SGPR def-use trees, scalar dynamic offsets and known root-view descriptors, build test oracle for every observed field; don't assume all five only need count.

Existing [`paired_fp32.rs`](../crates/victory-shader/src/emit/paired_fp32.rs) allows `VICTORY_WAVE_PAIRS_FP32=non-ieee-rne-ftz` only after per-program rejection of FP32 MODE mutations and unknown ordered reductions; two water shaders' known MODE write touches bits 2–3 (FP64/FP16), not FP32 bits0–1/4–5. Initial FP32/IEEE mode and actual NVIDIA golden outputs **not independently proved**. Restrict `VICTORY_WAVE_PAIRS_ONLY=5dc3b25986622949,6f225b641c5b0708`; never blanket make other Wave64 shaders Wave32 or force mode globally. ### R56 — Missing shaders forensic research: five dynamic descriptor failures + 28 Wave64 PSOs (research-only, 2026-11-27)

> **Source pin**: this tree on 2026-11-27, owner's actual Run11 logfile and [R55](#r55--run11-owner-evidence--claudes-six-optimization-research-questions-correctness-gates-and-scoped-prs-research-only-2026-11-27), original AMD RDNA2 Table 37, Microsoft HLSL WaveSize specification and upstream issue/PR discussions. **No shader files received or executed, and no implementation claim.** This section corrects R55's earlier two-water-only whitelist assumption: the currently checked-in [`shader_cache.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/shader_cache.rs) `PAIRS_BY_DEFAULT` includes **all 28** rejected hashes when `VICTORY_WAVE_PAIRS_ONLY` is unset; Run11 has no `WAVE64-PAIRS-ONLY`, `SHADER-FRESH` or `DESC-WHY` lines, so current-code defaults cannot be assumed to have run then.

- [ ] **R56 / P0 collect exact shader inputs, not a multi-GB executable:** runtime [`pipeline.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline.rs) L140–185 already saves each failed original `.cdxil` when `VICTORY_DUMP_REFUSED=<dir>`; `VICTORY_SHADER_FRESH=002e93a6cc2aa355,07c93bfb77fecebb,0e64f14fae060d04,96ccb14ef981fd85,dbdbf36298727135 VICTORY_DESC_WHY=1` forces serial fresh diagnostics. Ask owner for only these **five** small `.cdxil` files + short associated log privately if sharing permitted. The two translated-but-PSO-refused water shaders require offline `VICTORY_TRANSLATE_ONLY=5dc3b25986622949,6f225b641c5b0708` with `VICTORY_DUMP_MATCH=<dir>;#5dc3...|#6f225...` and `victory translate <shader-archive> --list --jobs 1` ([`translate.rs`](../../apps/victory/src/translate.rs) L390–449); `--warm` does not dump. Avoid EXE and game archive until these are proved unobtainable.
- [ ] **R56 / P0 map five exact Run11 translation failures**: `002e93a6cc2aa355` PC `0x1d70`, `07c93bfb77fecebb` `0x2070`, `0e64f14fae060d04` `0x1e18`, `96ccb14ef981fd85` `0x1e70`, `dbdbf36298727135` `0x1e48`. All compute, identical `a descriptor read only as data` rejection; this is `Lower::descriptor_data` final **unknown-kind/provenance** path at [`emit/lower.rs`](../crates/victory-shader/src/emit/lower.rs) L6177, **before** any descriptor bits are rebuilt. Verify per-PC raw `s_load_dwordx4` instruction, `Address{param,offset,dynamic}`, RootType and transitive SGPR dword0..3 consumers. `Address.param` is index into `native.layout` ([`resource.rs`](../crates/victory-shader/src/resource.rs) L24–34), not necessarily the host root slot. Do **not** assert all five use a V# descriptor, `NUM_RECORDS` or the same source; hash `07c93b...` was independently named Full TraceShadowRays in [#201](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/201#issuecomment-7187356019), but that fork progressed to an entirely different Wave64 blocker.
- [ ] **R56 / P0 identify the missing dynamic descriptor path**: `descriptor_data` looks for direct `Role::Resource` use, a `twin` with identical `Address` and opcode (dynamic SSA `Value` exact identity only), or `unused_descriptor_kind` (4-dword only if dword2 is live, 8-dword image with dimension uses). Then `view_address` explicitly requires `rp.root==Table && a.dynamic.is_none() && a.offset%32==0 && used.dwords==4` ([`lower.rs`](../crates/victory-shader/src/emit/lower.rs) L2804–2835). Dynamic heap/table selection cannot use this fixed-table fallback. Determine **which condition failed for each hash**, and how consumed DWORDs flow through copies, phis, waterfall lane selection, pointer arithmetic, format tests or loop bounds. Fix provenance first if another load points to the same entry; avoid guessed kind/zeros.
- [ ] **R56 / P0 prototype guest descriptor metadata, NOT D3D12 handle reinterpretation**: the guest's virtualized title heap contains `vVIE` records (and view generations), not raw AMD buffer descriptor words ([`descriptor.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/descriptor.rs) L1–6,L694–815), and `sync_heaps` mirrors raw title record copies ([same file](../crates/victory-gpu/src/descriptor.rs) L173–265). Candidate: preserve/synthesize **guest-visible** 4 DWORD buffer metadata separately from **host ResourceDescriptorHeap handle**, indexed by exact per-lane dynamic slot, updated on create/copy/mirror/release/remap and fence-safe. Upstream **[#2363 OPEN](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/2363)** already does 16-byte/slot address, record count, format/type on an older `view_addresses` path (external descriptor failures 37→0), **but its compute totals are unchanged and it zeroes some dword1 high bits**, so it is NOT a proven fix for these 5 or stride reads. The current tree has `SLOT_STRIDES` metadata ([`emit/shader.rs`](../crates/victory-shader/src/emit/shader.rs)) and fixed `USER_VIEW_ADDRESS` precedent; new table costs 32MiB for `2^21` slots if unconditional, VRAM pressure must be measured. Cross-check [#1130](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/1130) (same-entry semantic provenance, closed unmerged), [#1845](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/1845) (high-bit-stride proof + unrelated bundled patches, open), [#798](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/798) (descriptor word used as another SMEM base) and [#1482](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/1482) (dual representations).
- [ ] **R56 / P0 validate exact guest DWORD uses from primary docs**: [AMD RDNA2 buffer resource descriptor Table 37](https://www.amd.com/content/dam/amd/en/documents/radeon-tech-docs/instruction-set-architectures/rdna2-shader-instruction-set-architecture.pdf) and [Mesa GFX10 descriptor construction](https://chromium.googlesource.com/external/gitlab.freedesktop.org/mesa/mesa/+/418c4cfa6708a0e0b1175e72fb8fd27d3ca1615a/src/amd/common/ac_descriptors.c): D0 base VA low32; D1 base VA high16 + 14-bit stride + swizzle; D2 record count (bytes for stride0, records for stride>0); D3 format/component map/OOB/type. `s_load_dwordx4` is a generic 16-byte SMEM read and does **not** prove those are descriptor words. Test raw pointer arithmetic, dword1 upper stride, dword2 bounds/division, dword3 format and malformed/null descriptors against independent expected guest results. No silent `undef`/0 even when DXIL validator passes.
- [ ] **R56 / P0 audit all 28 REAL Wave64 PSO failures**, distinct from five translation failures. Run11 host PSO errors `0x80070057`, `WaveSize(64)` on 32-lane RTX4070: 14 with group `[64,1,1]`, 7 `[8,8,1]`, 2 each `[128,1,1]`, `[512,1,1]`, `[1024,1,1]`, 1 `[256,1,1]`. Microsoft [SM6.6 WaveSize](https://microsoft.github.io/DirectX-Specs/d3d/HLSL_SM_6_6_WaveSize.html) guarantees PSO rejection if requested size exceeds card support. Need per-hash guest wave semantics, LDS/barriers, bitmask, first-active-lane, derivative/FP reductions and game dispatch count; runtime PSO creation/SDK validation is **not** enough to prove correct outputs. Compare [#439](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/439), [#2176](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/2176), [#2711](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/2711), [#2925](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/2925); don't blindly enable approximate host Wave32.
- [ ] **R56 / P0 hostile water masked-loop test before any default flip**: The owner has prior `diagnostics/translate-water-5dc3-6f22-2026-11-26.txt` with cross-half LDS, partial EXEC and first-lane PCs. Current `paired_fp32.rs` accepts `non-ieee-rne-ftz` only with a narrow MODE proof, but this does NOT establish initial guest mode, FP64 reductions, NaN priorities or exit-lane phi semantics. **New external adversarial [#3502 comment 7218705676](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/3502#issuecomment-7218705676)**: both water PSOs **created** on RTX5080 using #2633+#2978+#385, yet **192/192 guest lanes computed wrong first-iteration FP64 sums** because the two 32-lane halves did not exchange complete contributions; a straight-line fix did not resolve masked-loop SSA carry. Require native64 or independent CPU golden outputs for 64-bit reductions/partial EXEC/LDS/first-active lane; do not equate valid DXIL with exact water.
- [ ] **R56 / P1 controlled classification and acceptance gates:** once seven `.cdxil` files arrive, record `GFX10 PC, root layout source, SGPR use-by-DWORD, exact fields, translated flag, dxil.dll validator, RTX4070 PSO, dispatch read-back, screenshot, DRED` per hash. Categorize A semantic sibling; B record-count only; C guest pointer/secondary SMEM base; D stride/format data; E code-relative constants or mixed phi. Keep owner `BVH_BUILDER=0` during game validation until separate null-write crash is resolved. Require zero newly incorrect prior shaders, shader cache key segregation, fence-safe descriptor metadata and local VRAM budget.

### R57 — ORIGINAL GAME SHADER BINARY FORENSICS: exact five shadow variants, ResourceHeap guest BVH address and Wave64 (verified corpus, research-only, 2026-11-27)

> **PRIVATE ORIGINAL-BYTES EVIDENCE:** owner provided `shaders(1).rpf` (411,695,600 B, SHA256 `0bc1634594e51ca8e7ef5f2bc6f5b32b081ce779ac094d686de08ecb75dc24a8`), XboxBvhBuilder ZIP + other game archives. Independently parsed **26,503** valid SGD5 raw-deflate records per [`sgd5.rs`](../crates/victory-game/src/sgd5.rs), compared original decompressed DXBC BLAKE3 prefixes exactly against Run11; **all five missing shader containers AND both water shader containers physically found**. Private source assets are **NOT committed or attached here**. **Research evidence and proposed fix only:** the translator/SDK/RTX/scene have not been run with a patch. Detailed text-only handoff `Defeat_R57_Verified_Shadow_Shader_Binary_Forensics_Claude_Handoff_2026-11-27.txt`, companion 5-row CSV, source-cited tests and falsification cases saved as downloadable conversation artifacts, not in repo.

- [ ] **R57 / P0 FIVE ARE ONE SHADOW-RAY FAMILY, not five random opcodes.** All five CS programs have 229 native layout entries, 47 `UsedResource` entries, **`native.layout[228]={root:ResourceHeap(5),user_data:6,sgprs:1,slot:0}`**, and dynamically indexed `s_load_dwordx4` through root heap address SGPR `s6:s7`. Each reads **guest buffer descriptor DWORD0/DWORD1 as raw BVH pointer/address bits**, e.g. DWORD1 `& 0xFFFF`, 64bit `>>8` (Full also DWORD0 `&0xFFFFFF00`, DWORD1 `&0xFF`). Real original GFX10 and log PCs by hash:
  - `96ccb14ef981fd85`: quality 1/8, `[8,4,1]` Wave32; SMEM **0x1de8**, downstream Run11 failure **0x1e70** (SOPP branch).
  - `0e64f14fae060d04`: quality 1/4, `[8,4,1]` Wave32; SMEM **0x1d90**, failure **0x1e18** (branch).
  - `002e93a6cc2aa355`: quality 1/2, `[4,8,1]` Wave32; SMEM **0x1ce8**, failure **0x1d70** (branch).
  - `07c93bfb77fecebb`: full, `[8,8,1]` guest **Wave64**; SMEM **0x1d44**, failure **0x2070 = `image_bvh64_intersect_ray`** (MIMG opcode `0x0e7`), plus separate Nvidia 32-lane-host gate.
  - `dbdbf36298727135`: adaptive, `[4,8,1]` Wave32; SMEM **0x1dc0**, failure **0x1e48** (branch).
  These are **native instruction PCs**, NOT RPF file offsets; Run11's failure PC is an SSA *consumer* after the dynamic SMEM load, not the `s_load` itself. Inspect per-load GFX10 reachable decode+SSA trace; don't treat every aligned 4-byte word as executable code.
- [ ] **R57 / P0 FOUR NEGATIVE-CONTROL SHADOW VARIANTS:** `559d9f0184e15fe3`, `4e816e1ef600ddcf`, `eb9a057a81d18c42`, `e6ac855a4cddb16d`: same shader family, native layouts **221/221/222/222** and used resources **29/29/36/37**, **no corresponding s6-based dynamically offset `s_load_dwordx4` motif** found. The five missing share 229/47 + root228 ResourceHeap. Positive/negative comparison strongly favors a particular dynamic BVH descriptor path, not an unknown GFX10 opcode or a single `getDimensions` omission. No game rendering comparison was run.
- [ ] **R57 / P0 EXACT TRANSLATOR-SOURCE DISCONNECT:** [`resource.rs`](../crates/victory-shader/src/resource.rs) `Address.param=228` indexes `NativeProgram.layout`, **not** a host root slot. [`emit/lower.rs`](../crates/victory-shader/src/emit/lower.rs) `user(ResourceHeap)` maps guest heap-base to zero (L2674), `heap_index` **already handles dynamic ResourceHeap** (L6999) to make an opaque host D3D12 handle, but `descriptor_data` (L6042–6393) cannot infer a resource kind from absent used-kind/twin; `unused_descriptor_kind` for four DWORDs only recognizes **DWORD2 record-count** reads, while five need **DWORD0/DWORD1 guest VA**. `view_address` (L2804–2835) explicitly requires `RootType::Table` plus `dynamic.is_none()` and thus rejects all five's dynamic `ResourceHeap` address loads. The return `Err("a descriptor read only as data")` at L6177 matches Run11. **Fix provenance and guest VA representation, not a dummy 0 or dimensions query.**
- [ ] **R57 / P0 PROPOSED NARROW FIX for Claude (not implemented):** add an independent **GPU-readable guest descriptor metadata** path for dynamically indexed `ResourceHeap` buffer and acceleration-structure views, keyed by exactly the **same host heap index/dynamic-slot mapping** as `heap_index`. Reconstruct **guest base VA low32/high16** for each slot (and stride high14 / record count / format only if actual consumer requires and values are faithful). Underlying [`descriptor.rs`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/descriptor.rs) stores **virtual `vVIE` tagged records and host-side D3D12 view descriptions**, not original raw RDNA2 buffer DWORDs: never reinterpret/copy their dwords as original console descriptor. Existing [`draw.rs::host_word(USER_VIEW_ADDRESS)`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs) and [`descriptor/host.rs::buffer_range`](../crates/victory-gpu/src/descriptor/host.rs) provide **fixed-table guest-address precedent**; extend dynamic selection without conflating guest VA, D3D12 handle, GPU virtual address or title descriptor *record*. `2^21` host slots × 2 DWORDs = **16 MiB** theoretical metadata (×4=32 MiB), plus lifetime allocations; measure 12GB card VRAM.
- [ ] **R57 / P0 MUST HANDLE BVH AS `Location`**: independent upstream **[#2098](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/pull/2098) OPEN** shows a NULL-resource `D3D12_SRV_DIMENSION_RAYTRACING_ACCELERATION_STRUCTURE` view's actual BVH VA comes from the view description's `Location`, not `buffer_range` (which returns None/zero). Its external address mask pattern mirrors these shaders. Guest metadata must choose **ordinary buffer's effective first-element address OR AS Location**, plus explicit valid null/stale handling. Without #2098-style AS case, a translator can emit while silently tracing title address 0.

[AMD RDNA2 ISA Table37](https://www.amd.com/content/dam/amd/en/documents/radeon-tech-docs/instruction-set-architectures/rdna2-shader-instruction-set-architecture.pdf) formally defines D0 base VA low32, D1 VA high16/stride14, D2 record count, D3 format/control.
- [ ] **R57 / P1 separate other shader populations:** recovered water `5dc3b25986622949`, `6f225b641c5b0708` both native Wave64 `[8,8,1]`, no descriptor-only first failure. Uploaded XboxBvhBuilder DLL contains **52 embedded native compute shaders: 42 Wave32 + 10 Wave64**; **none overlap Run11's 28 PSO-refused hashes**; independent builder and null-write stability gate (owner README). Do not equate valid water/Full PSOs with exact output: [#3502 masked-loop test](https://github.com/Marvisak/VIctory/issues/3502#issuecomment-7218705676) produced **192/192 wrong first-iteration FP64 wave sums** despite successful RTX PSO creation. Other uploaded archives contain no *valid raw SGD5 records* found; that does not exclude other internal formats.
