Other preliminary readback comparison 3.52→4.05 fps in matched interiors but mixed outdoor outcomes. | **Classify read vs write exactly** before copying bytes or declaring them dirty. Defeat already tracks GPU access flags/dirtiness and has shader code very different from DexNom; **investigate whether the particular error exists before any source modification**. RX7900XTX 24GB/Intel and RT-enabled workload are not matched. |

#### B. Inspected code differences worth researching before porting

| Priority and lead | Exact primary source and demonstrated design | Defeat overlap / remaining actual test | Stop/go; visual or memory failure mode |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P0 ARCHITECTURE: thin guest list capture + worker translation** | [NVIDIA `list/thin.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/list/thin.rs): `VICTORY_THIN=1` serializes D3D12 guest calls/pointed-at parameters as small ordered records, then translates to host lists on worker threads while guest continues; chunks of 1,024 words, later queue submission. Source motivates 45–60k list calls/frame and 160–190ms **CPU time over guest recording threads** in one NVIDIA-fork workload. | Defeat [`list.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/list.rs) presently records directly onto host lists; it already has [async submission](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/queue.rs), per-thread prepare, QUEUE2 and worker texture refresh—**not the same as deferring command-method translation**. R67 measures substantial queue-empty/submitter time but does not isolate call-translation vs title CPU work. | **First:** measure per-method wall time, guest call counts, capture/snapshot bytes and GPU queue ready gaps in one matched scene. Then synthetic trace/replay with list reset/reuse, pointer arguments, descriptor overwrite, alias remap, fences and wait-in-list cuts; byte/picture parity before any opt-in runtime A/B. No new global default. |
| **P0/P1 GPU-list submission/descriptor overlap** | [NVIDIA `host/aside.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host/aside.rs) asynchronously computes heap-view refreshes, then joins at list close; [`heap_marks.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/heap_marks.rs) can begin descriptor sync at title Submit with dependency-mark handoff (ported from Belobug per source). | Defeat already has `VICTORY_HEAP_WORKER`, `VICTORY_PARALLEL_REFRESH` and host slot mirroring; it **does not automatically need duplicate workers**. Compare its [`draw.rs` descriptor stats](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs) and preparation timeline with first-list close vs actual GPU enqueue. | Prove >5ms on *non-overlapping* recording→submit critical path before trial; preserve descriptor generation, list lifetime, waits after guest writes and first-visible texture readiness. |
| **P0/P1 guarded title-write bypass and fewer kernel/protection calls** | [NVIDIA `titlewrite.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/titlewrite.rs): opt-in `VICTORY_COPY_BESIDE` overrides guest `memcpy/memmove/memset` imports on guarded ranges; `VICTORY_STORE_BESIDE` decodes selected faulting stores and writes via a parallel unguarded mapping, recording write state. [`safecopy.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/safecopy.rs) offers AVX2 32-byte compare/merge plus recoverable fault path. | Run16 R67 guard all-thread median wait ~51.1ms in >=3k frames; [Defeat `watch.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/watch.rs) already uses guarded-memory notifications; [`plaincopy.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/plaincopy.rs) already implements `rep movsb` fault-and-fallback and recent `0c62860` word-run tests. **Do not reimplement fast copy or assume full 51ms is on critical path.** | First use existing guard caller wait/hold and write histogram. Model `memmove` overlap, partial/unmapped pages, writes racing map/unmap, vector/store instruction decode, watch re-arm and exception-handler nesting. An import hook that alters title instruction semantics or misses write journal is NOT acceptable. Bounded opt-in fixture only. |
| **P1 scene-cut small-buffer preallocation** | [NVIDIA `ahead.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/ahead.rs): worker precreates 64KiB mapped/VRAM buffers; source reports 3,450 created on demand cost ~2.8s of 4s first-world frame on RTX4070 SUPER/7800X3D; `VICTORY_BUFFERS_AHEAD` defaults 4096, retains up to 1024. | Defeat creates host buffers on demand; no matching equivalent was established by source audit. **4096×64KiB ≈256MiB per side before overhead; card copies plus mapped buffers may press RTX4070 budget**, with R67 headroom proxy 352MB in some windows. New alloc/residency can help transitions while worsening VRAM eviction/stutter. | First count CREATE calls/size/time per world transition and sample actual `QueryVideoMemoryInfo` budget. If >5ms critical-path recurring, synthetic **small bounded** prealloc (e.g. 64–256 resources) under free-budget admission, mark default OFF; prove unused pool released, no device loss, warm startup not slower. No blind 4096 default. |
| **P1 texture allocation pool and staging pressure** | [NVIDIA `pool.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/pool.rs) uses 64MiB blocks, 2.5s reuse rest, budget-aware spare-block count; [`residency.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/residency.rs) samples physical and commit headroom, backs off new upload-block attempts after OOM. Fork source reports allocation reduction from ~310µs to ~6µs per small piece on 7900XTX, **not an RTX4070 measurement**. [fxceless's budget gate](https://github.com/fxceless/victory-fork/blob/main/README.md) reports prior 0.8–1GB over-budget stalls in long runs. | Defeat has sparse level-on-demand slots, budget/room tracking, in-flight resource lifetime and [placement observer](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/placement.rs), not confirmed identical 64MiB texture/staging pools. R67 VRAM nearly full; there is no spare 4GiB for speculation. | Profile per-resource CreateCommittedResource/Copy footprint/fence wait and VRAM headroom. Bounded pool trial with exact allocation alignment and **fence-completed retirement** (never assume 2.5s alone proves safe); count failed allocations and real budget before and after. Stop at any memory regression/device removal. |
| **P1 correct material-driven texture lookahead** | [DexNom `material_demand.rs`](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/material_demand.rs) `VICTORY_MATERIAL_TEXTURE_DEMAND=1` collects a few explicitly known shader-material offsets/slots, generation checks, and bounded next-frame hints. [`texture/ahead.rs`](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/texture/ahead.rs) `VICTORY_UPLOAD_AHEAD=1` conditionally uploads immutable snapshots on a copy queue with armed/ready/discard/fence state (96MiB cap). | Defeat already has texture worker, QUEUE2, `VICTORY_LEVELS_ON_DEMAND`, sparse tiles and on-demand GPU residency. Do not duplicate heap worker or use stale material indices from another shader translator. R67 changed-after-upload warnings suggest **correctness gate before speed**. | First trace *one delayed visible texture* request→descriptor write→guest memory stamp→upload fence→first drawn. Same-camera pixel/generation parity mandatory; reject prefetch if occupancy, hidden stale slot or extra memory pressure outweighs avoided stall. |
| **P1/P2 texture small-resource heap packing** | [DexNom `resource/small.rs`](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/resource/small.rs) packs eligible tiny single-plane 2D read-only resources with 4KiB placement alignment into distinct offsets of 64KiB heaps; checks D3D12 allocation information and excludes unsupported formats. | Defeat uses heaps/tiles for other needs; **measure actual small committed-texture count and 64KiB slack first**. Bandwidth may not change, but budget pressure may. | Target only qualifying formats/resource flags, sample count/slack by size, retain alias-free offsets and lifetime; test debug-layer + WARP + RTX4070 exact host allocation sizes. Reject if estimated savings are negligible or resource aliasing/sampler lifetime risks emerge. |
| **P1 copy-back classification; do not blindly skip** | [NVIDIA `land.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/land.rs) groups 64KiB pieces (~256KiB job) for multi-threaded safe merging, including own-view writes, with output-order rules. [`host/traffic.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host/traffic.rs) counts per-copy upload vs readback traffic. [DexNom `writeback.rs`](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/writeback.rs) tracks pending write owners across remaps and publication; [`memory/deferred_readback_tests.rs`](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/memory/deferred_readback_tests.rs) is a **synthetic demand shadow, not retail deferred readback**. | Defeat already parallelizes card copy-back, has exact/own-view merges, shadow generations and `VICTORY_READBACK_REUSE=1` **observer**. Run16 p50 copy-back fell to 43.3ms from 76ms in unlike scenes; further gain possible but don't erase this tested baseline. | Owner must obtain actual CPU read/IO consumer before safe skipped GPU→CPU work; multithread remaps, readback under guest write and non-tile merges must preserve **every guest-visible byte**. Observe one-way traffic first. No blanket `WC_NO_BACK`, `GPU_OWNED`, or lazy readback from fork's defaults. |
| **P1/P2 replay and CPU/GPU clock synchronization** | [NVIDIA `timeline/record.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/timeline/record.rs) captures bounded thread/fence/submit/guest poll spans; [`host/stamps.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host/stamps.rs) maps calibrated D3D12 queue timestamps to CPU QPC. [`docs/replay.md`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/docs/replay.md) records and replays **one complete frame** and compares images/pipeline timings; report says 3–10ms recording overhead, a 9GB sample capture and 5.4s paused capture in one scenario. | Defeat already has `cardmeter`, existing detailed `VICTORY_GPU_TIMING`, `xtask runstats` and capture support, but **not this exact reusable replay tool**. A 9GB capture and GPU wait are expensive on a 12GB card. | Prioritize **lightweight 2–3-frame calibrated event trace**, then offline reduced synthetic replay for measured critical path. Full capture only after budget/disk/asset privacy check; never benchmark native FPS in capture mode. |
| **P2 speculative compute queue/soft views** | [NVIDIA `host/two_queues.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host/two_queues.rs) is **`VICTORY_ASYNC_QUEUE=plan` only**; it explicitly notes all host lists remain direct and many texture/resource states cannot move onto compute. Its `VICTORY_SOFT_VIEWS` launcher setting avoids some Windows mapping scene-cut freezes but depends on different guest-page mapping semantics. | Defeat already runs D3D12 direct/extra copy queues with original console fence order. Another compute queue is NOT already a known win; scene-cut soft view may conflict with exact physical aliases/write guards. | Require plan proving resource state access, indirect lists, fences, remaps and D3D12 command-list legality, then timeline estimate of genuinely independent GPU work; stop if cards remain underutilized due CPU prepare. |
| **P3 / visuals-only mode** | [NVIDIA `streamline.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/streamline.rs) DLSS Super Resolution/Frame Generation; [foxhound README](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/docs/README-foxhound.md) confirms **approximate ambient/reflections**, skipped effects, 30fps title cap and no RT in high-FPS measurements. | DLSS FG can create more presented frames without reducing 168ms native frame latency; VRAM overhead can starve textures. Rendering at lower internal resolution may matter only after GPU work shown to be bottleneck. | Preserve FPS accounting **native rendered / generated displayed / game simulation** distinctly; optional quality-perf modes need clearly labeled screenshots and owner approval. Never silently trade missing effects for a claimed equivalent FPS improvement. |
| **P2 shader and RT correctness separately** | [DexNom ray policy](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/pipeline/ray_tracing.rs), [benchmark](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/BENCHMARK.md): five shadow pipelines make on RX7900XTX and RT BVH walker sees ray hits; [fxceless README](https://github.com/fxceless/victory-fork/blob/main/README.md) documents some 32-lane wave/water corrections and RT path limitations. | NVIDIA RTX4070's refused WaveSize(64) compute programs and Defeat's missing fifth shadow shader are distinct: a PSO validated on an RDNA GPU **does not validate** NVDA 32-lane mapping. Builder OFF is consistent with this run's performance focus. | Per-hash DXIL validator/compiler/wave lane and UAV lifetime parity with source. Do not bundle shader/RT redesign into the CPU-FPS workstream. |

**Key case that is *not* an equivalent direct win:** NVIDIA [`safecopy.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/safecopy.rs) replaces many `ReadProcessMemory` calls with an own-fault handler and SIMD compare, but Defeat already has [`plaincopy.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/plaincopy.rs) using an analogous faulting `rep movsb` fast path and [`shadow.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) word-run batching. **Do not assign a generic 'implement safe copy' task**. Investigate genuinely different AVX2 word-mask merge or redundant copies with same-byte and source-generation proof, only if Run17 attribution favors them.

#### C. Other emulators inspected — transferable principles, not ports

| Primary source (inspected) | Real mechanism | Defeat-specific interpretation |
|---|---|---|
| [Xenia Canary D3D12 command processor](https://github.com/xenia-canary/xenia-canary/blob/canary_experimental/src/xenia/gpu/d3d12/d3d12_command_processor.cc) and [shared memory](https://github.com/xenia-canary/xenia-canary/blob/canary_experimental/src/xenia/gpu/d3d12/d3d12_shared_memory.cc) | Optional earlier submit at primary buffer end, shared-memory upload/state tracking and tiled D3D12 resources; original platform **Xbox 360**. | Model **safe earlier submit** using Defeat's existing async submit, guest order, alias/fence generations. Do not transfer PM4 packet cutoff or Xbox360 cache strategy unchanged. |
| [RPCS3 RSX texture cache](https://github.com/RPCS3/rpcs3/blob/master/rpcs3/Emu/RSX/Common/texture_cache.h), [predictor](https://github.com/RPCS3/rpcs3/blob/master/rpcs3/Emu/RSX/Common/texture_cache_predictor.h) | Tracks cache sections/invalidation causes, flush/unprotect exclusions, predicts range and format reuse. | Offline property-test **RO/RW/CPU-GPU dirty state** and the exact condition for flushing CPU-visible data; existing Defeat guards and dirty journals mean no blanket deferred `VirtualProtect`. |
| [shadPS4 GPU/CPU memory tracker](https://github.com/shadps4-emu/shadPS4/blob/main/src/video_core/buffer_cache/memory_tracker.h) | Separate modified ranges for CPU and GPU and per-range upload/download enumerators; 2026 release notes describe batched uploads. | Model generation-aware per-page ownership and true consumer-bound data movement, but note Defeat guest physical aliases and DirectStorage completion are not shadPS4's Vulkan sync semantics. |
| [Dolphin FIFO player](https://github.com/dolphin-emu/dolphin/blob/master/Source/Core/Core/FifoPlayer/FifoPlayer.cpp), [performance guide](https://dolphin-emu.org/docs/guides/performance-guide/) | Replays recorded graphics commands for analysis; shader precompile and hybrid ubershader vs skip-draw tradeoffs; guide warns skipping EFB CPU copies can break game logic. | Reinforces **GPU replay** and **do not skip guest reads**. Defeat already disk-caches shader translations/PSOs and drops draws for disabled builder; generic Dolphin ubershaders do not directly replace GFX10 DXIL. |
| [xemu NV2A Vulkan texture cache](https://github.com/xemu-project/xemu/blob/master/hw/xbox/nv2a/pgraph/vk/texture.c) and [OpenGL surface downloads](https://github.com/xemu-project/xemu/blob/master/hw/xbox/nv2a/pgraph/gl/surface.c) | Texture-cache resource lifetimes, conditional downloads of GPU-written surfaces while respecting guest CPU-visible state. | Source-audit which guest CPU accesses require readback, not remove all; original Xbox NV2A uses a different tiling/texture state and performance scale. |
| [Cemu Vulkan renderer](https://github.com/cemu-project/Cemu/blob/main/src/Cafe/HW/Latte/Renderer/Vulkan/VulkanRenderer.cpp) and [pipeline compiler](https://github.com/cemu-project/Cemu/blob/main/src/Cafe/HW/Latte/Renderer/Vulkan/VulkanPipelineCompiler.cpp) | Pipeline caching/compilation and recorded command execution are explicit; Wii U/Latte + Vulkan. | Source guide for diagnosing cold-pipeline stutter, not a priority over Defeat's **Run16 near-zero pipeline waits** for typical >=3k windows. |
| [Vita3K Vulkan texture cache](https://github.com/Vita3K/Vita3K/blob/master/vita3k/renderer/src/vulkan/texture.cpp) | Tracks reusable staging buffers, fences and texture-cache lifetime for the PlayStation Vita GPU. | Study **bounded staging reuse**, but no evidence the same layouts, sizes or scheduler overheads apply to Defeat's D3D12 Xbox Series target. |
| [DuckStation hardware GPU](https://github.com/stenzek/duckstation/blob/master/src/core/gpu_hw.cpp) | Has explicit GPU state/cache and rendering techniques, but PS1 GPU semantics and resource sizes are dramatically different. | **Low priority P3**; avoid porting graphics-specific optimizations as if interchangeable. |

**Vendor guardrails:** [Microsoft D3D12 residency](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/direct3d12/residency) explains default heap priority, memory-budget notification and how moving the wrong resource out of local VRAM can worsen latency; [DXGI video-memory query](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/dxgi1_4/ns-dxgi1_4-dxgi_query_video_memory_info) warns that `CurrentUsage > Budget` may cause OS-mediated stutters. The RTX4070's near-full observed budget makes texture pools, render-target reserves, framegen and extra GPU_UPLOAD allocations **competitive for the same memory**. No global priority flip, old-staging reclamation by time alone, or automatic migration without original owner/alias/fence verification. **Licensing:** the VIctory forks and third-party emulators differ in source licences; independently explain principles and check licence compatibility/attribution with Claude before any actual code reuse.

#### D. Actionable cross-emulator research queue (what to give Claude vs Gemini)

- [ ] **R68/P0-1 — CLAUDE offline profiling, then owner single-game baseline:** use already committed `runstats`, guard logs, descriptor table counts and cardmeter; add **bounded** code-path attribution only if existing counters cannot split a heavy frame into (a) title's guest command-call time, (b) direct host call translation, (c) list ready→queue execute, (d) descriptor/heap pass, (e) guest page/guard hold vs wait, (f) copy-back and exact GPU spans. **Done when:** one matched 4,500–5,500 and 5,500–7,000-draw segment shows N, p50/p90, large-frame outlier owners, source SHA, normal switches, screenshot parity, and at least one >5ms **non-overlapping** critical path cost identified. Never estimate from sums of median thread time.
- [ ] **R68/P0-2 — CLAUDE source-level thin-translation feasibility proof, no global port:** map Defeat `list.rs` / `draw.rs` / `queue.rs` call/read/write semantics against NVIDIA `list/thin.rs`; count pointer-valued arguments that need deep snapshot, descriptor slots rewritten mid-list and command lists cut by guest GPU waits. Synthetic reference replay of list reset/reuse, early pipeline binding, UAV/CBV, indirect, remap, page map, async submit and fence status. **Done when:** explicit state diagram, sample frame cost/queue-stall budget, deterministic byte/pixel parity; a minimal separately disabled candidate may be considered by Claude **only if** a meaningful work component can move off the critical path.
- [ ] **R68/P0-3 — CLAUDE guard/descriptor wait provenance:** compare Defeat's [`descriptor/host.rs` dynamic slot refusal](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/descriptor/host.rs) and [`draw.rs` lock timings](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs) with fxceless dynamic wait path and NVIDIA title write imports. **Done when:** separate `free slot`, `no contiguous run`, `lock hold`, `waiting for landing`, `VirtualProtect` and `DirectStorage before-write` costs recorded on Run17-type diagnostic. A bulk import/fault bypass is blocked until protection, aliases and title CPU store semantics are exhaustively tested; never trade dropped draws for unbounded stalls.
- [ ] **R68/P1-4 — CLAUDE 12GB memory/transition gate:** use current Defeat allocation/room counters and 4070 budget/commit telemetry to compare scene-cut **small-buffer creation**, tiny host textures' 64KiB alignment slack, texture creation latency and staging-block retention against NVIDIA `ahead.rs`/`pool.rs` and DexNom `resource/small.rs`. **Done when:** actual per-frame allocations and clock costs, budget at creation, resident bytes retained, and one bounded VRAM-safe offline test justify any code work. Do not preallocate 4096 resources on a card with a few hundred MB reported slack.
- [ ] **R68/P1-5 — CLAUDE texture visibility/generation gate:** take the R67 black/late textures, slot mutation warnings, and physical-page remaps and run a single-asset first-visible timeline; compare DexNom `material_demand.rs` and `texture/ahead.rs` immutable-snapshot cancellation/fence approach. **Done when:** one asset class has reproducible CPU/IO/GPU bottleneck and stable screenshot, with pending texturing/alias correctness accounted for. No blind copy queue or stale material ID lookahead.
- [ ] **R68/P1-6 — CLAUDE observe potential GPU readback avoidance only:** Defeat `VICTORY_READBACK_REUSE=1` is already built but absent from Run16; measure it separately with zero picture changes, duration/p95 overhead and distinction of own-view vs old-way copies. Then prove **actual CPU read / storage status consumer** and remap generation (GPU-written data must not disappear before a guest read). **Done when:** bound and false-positive rate documented; no user-facing lazy-readback implementation before a correct observation model.
- [ ] **R68/P2-7 — GEMINI deep technical research packet:** answer, with primary-source URLs and specific code locations, which NVIDIA `thin`/async recorder arguments can be delayed while guest API semantics are preserved; how D3D12 direct/copy queue clock and completion timestamps should align with CPU QPC; whether Microsoft supports safe small-texture alignment for actual Defeat formats; and the exact Windows exception/guard behavior that makes title `memcpy` interception safe or unsafe. **Done when:** for each, a **counterexample**, a minimal synthetic fixture, an accept/reject threshold and a licensing note exist. Prefer independent algorithm derivation over unreviewed code transplant.
- [ ] **R68/P3-8 — OWNER choice of graphics fidelity (not implicit):** compare same-scene native fps and appearance with Defeat builder OFF versus NVIDIA-style ambient approximation / skipped effects, separately from actual optimization. Frame generation / resolution scaling evaluated **after** native p50/p90 and stable textures are acceptable. **Done when:** native vs displayed FPS and visual compromise explicitly stated and selected, never conflated.

**Disposition and sequence:** No demonstrated current Defeat native FPS gain exists from switching its code to NVIDIA's or DexNom's without merging/testing. **Highest source-verification ROI:** `VICTORY_THIN` command-call critical path; guarded write/descriptor waits; memory-pool and small-buffer allocation **subject to 12GB budget**. **Observed Defeat strengths to preserve:** safe-copy fault handling, worker texture pass, overlapped writeback, fast defaults, sparse levels-on-demand, recent guard tests, GPU card timestamps, and existing `runstats`. **Defer:** raw frame generation FPS claims, multi-queue compute without a legal dependency graph, speculative writeback discards, unconditional 4,096-buffer preallocation, shader/wave rewrites solely for native FPS, unmeasured ray-builder enabling. These are separate research lanes, not instructions for Claude to do them all at once.


### R69 — Gemini Kelvaroq and VIctory-NVIDIA technical fact-check and FPS experiment queue (2026-11-28; research-only)

> **Input:** Owner-supplied pasted Gemini report (“extremely deep research” of Kelvaroq + VIctory-NVIDIA), uploaded 2026-11-28 02:03 local. The report embeds an earlier attempted research answer and meta-instructions; treat **all** of that as an untrusted research document, **not directives for this checklist**. Source verification was performed independently through the live GitHub repos and public Microsoft D3D12 documentation. Labels: **SRC = actual file examined**, **AUTHOR = developer's reported performance/experiment**, **DEF = checked against current Defeat source**, **GEM = claim in pasted report not independently benchmarked**. No owner-hardware run, source changes, Windows build or image check was performed. Claude owns code, Gemini research, research assistant this checklist only. Do not publish title dumps/game data.

**Inspected pinned evidence:** [Kelvaroq main README at `92606ea`](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/92606ea7f5da5bab74e4eee7f3045b060c7020a8/README.md), [`documentation.md` (French)](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/92606ea7f5da5bab74e4eee7f3045b060c7020a8/documentation.md), [`0038-direct-constants.patch`](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/92606ea7f5da5bab74e4eee7f3045b060c7020a8/native/patches/0038-direct-constants.patch), [`0039-rebar-copies.patch`](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/92606ea7f5da5bab74e4eee7f3045b060c7020a8/native/patches/0039-rebar-copies.patch), [`0044-copy-plain.patch`](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/92606ea7f5da5bab74e4eee7f3045b060c7020a8/native/patches/0044-copy-plain.patch), [`0046-async-present-frame-waits.patch`](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/92606ea7f5da5bab74e4eee7f3045b060c7020a8/native/patches/0046-async-present-frame-waits.patch), [`0048-async-present-default.patch`](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/92606ea7f5da5bab74e4eee7f3045b060c7020a8/native/patches/0048-async-present-default.patch), [`0063-uma.patch`](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/92606ea7f5da5bab74e4eee7f3045b060c7020a8/native/patches/0063-uma.patch), [VIctory-NVIDIA main README](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/README.md), [foxhound original README](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/docs/README-foxhound.md) and [NVIDIA's card-side comparison implementation](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/crates/victory-gpu/src/host/card_steps.rs). **Correction of Gemini's retrieval disclaimer:** GitHub actually exposes full Kelvaroq `README.md`, full `documentation.md` and NVIDIA `docs/*` to this source audit; earlier inability to fetch does not make these claims unverifiable in principle.

#### A. What the Gemini report gets right, and where the evidence stops

| Report claim | Verification/classification | Practical Defeat interpretation |
|---|---|---|
| Kelvaroq runs Xbox Series x64 game code natively and translates graphics to D3D12 + DXIL; pins fxceless `f85101b` and ordered render patches | **SRC** Kelvaroq README, `documentation.md` and checked-in `native/patches/`. | Same VIctory ancestry; compare concrete call/ordering changes, not a CPU core emulator port. |
| Kelvaroq public heavy scene at 4K on RTX5080: patch groups gave **198.3ms p50** (0038–40 pre-mesh), **119.8ms** (0041–42), **108.5ms** (0044–48), latter **132.2ms mean / 7.6 mean fps / 261.5ms p95**, heavy windows defined >=3k draws | **AUTHOR**: README table supplies N **959 / 1564 / 1648** and claims equal save/warm cache, 300s, screenshot comparison. Different stages aggregate multiple code changes; no one-change attribution. | Strong evidence of feasibility for **classes** of work. Not an RTX4070 benchmark or proof the user gets the same delta. |
| Kelvaroq's GPU needed **22ms** for an open-world frame; “30fps open world” | **AUTHOR, EXPERIMENTAL**: current README explicitly corrected an earlier claim: the **35ms/30fps samples were loading/intro**, and **22ms is GPU-side work with data resident**, NOT completed game-frame or native open-world FPS. Public prologue still ~**7–10 rendered fps** by README. | GPU floor does not include Defeat's CPU preparation, remap/coherency, DirectStorage, copying/present, all of which matter to owner ~168ms frame p50. **Do not relay “Kelvaroq achieves stable open-world 30fps” as fact**. |
| Unified/direct-VRAM memory, ~64M/115M direct lookups, **97.4%** directly backed views, 121M descriptor rows with zero errors, native DXR implemented | **AUTHOR**, not independently reproduced. Public repository **does** include significant `0063-uma.patch` (source adds `inplace/pool.rs`, `memory/pool.rs`, `host/uma.rs` plus alias/bookkeeping tests), but README says new direct memory layer is **still being stabilized/cleaned** before public release. Publicly viewable patch != proof the advertised full experimental engine is shipping or is correct on RTX4070. The DXR claim is about a **build under test**, not a title-rendered, FPS-measured public release. | High-upside **long-term design comparison only**. Cross-system physical aliases, CPU read visibility, page remaps, GPU write publication/fences, tiled images, capacity and residency must be proved before any merged code. |
| Kelvaroq reports **~18s→~60ms** for write-time descriptor work, **~18s→~5ms** for bookkeeping (~3,000x) | **AUTHOR**: README reports **both** figures in different stages/wordings; the 18s is specifically **recorded-heavy-frame bookkeeping/replay**, not Defeat's live 168ms wall frame. ~18,000/60=300x and ~18,000/5=3,600x approximately; do not conflate these or imply ~18s was a current live game frame. | **Trace descriptors by exact write/rewrite source and generation** before considering a write-journal approach. Defeat already mirrors copied heap bytes and has `draw.rs` descriptor statistics; write-time-only invalidation must handle title bulk `memcpy`, GPU writes to descriptor records, address aliases and missed CPU faults. |
| Kelvaroq patch 0038 reads eligible descriptor constants directly off the GPU copy to avoid CPU fill waits | **SRC**: `VICTORY_DIRECT_CONSTANTS` patch accepts only safe span/size/alignment (256-byte hardware CBV alignment, copy length, shadow held and refreshed); falls back for other constants. `documentation.md` identifies prior 15 fills per frame waiting **170–590ms** on earlier GPU writes and a global `fill_ahead` flag susceptible to unnecessary broad waits. | **Most actionable novel lead; see P0-1 below.** Underlying problem may exist under changed Defeat timings, but R67 lacks a per-fill stall census. |
| Kelvaroq mesh-launch and wide-hash, plain host copy, async present, dual-queue, ReBAR improvements | **SRC** patches 0038–0048, documented combined before/after. **Already in Defeat:** `VICTORY_MESH_LAUNCH` ON by default, `VICTORY_CARD_HASH_WIDE` ON by default, `VICTORY_COPY_PLAIN` fast default, async submit/QUEUE2, optional `VICTORY_ASYNC_PRESENT`; separate GPU-upload/window support and card buffers exist. | **No generic “port mesh launch / wide hash / plaincopy / async present” task.** Verify modes and prior local perf; do not import a source-only fork switch without checking Defeat's equivalents. |
| VIctory-NVIDIA performance, GPU compares ~11→22 native fps, faster foxhound scenes, 2×/6× FG and VRAM budget | **AUTHOR** NVIDIA README explicitly says memory compares moved to card gave ~11→22fps on its tested base; foxhound README claims RTX4070 SUPER opening **27–30 native fps**, water ~21, builder off with alternate lighting; current fork RTX5070/9800X3D opening ~26–30 native fps. R68 independently documented source and caveats. GPU compare, wave mechanics, memory budget and FG are not a matched Defeat test. | Defeat already has GPU hash/compare, 64-thread wide hashes and card staging. **Do not repeat** NVIDIA deep dive unless the unported semantics have been named precisely. Frame generation changes displayed fps, not native simulation/compute time; its 12GB VRAM cost competes with textures. |

**Direct Defeat reconciliation (checked current code, no source edits):**

1. [Defeat `draw.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs) around table-constant handling **does not have** Kelvaroq's universal `VICTORY_DIRECT_CONSTANTS` mode: if `shaders_wrote(address,size)` and opt-in card-fill path succeeds, it uses `list.card_fill`; otherwise it calls `list.scratch` then `list.defer_fill` and `host.constant_at`. Distinguish **CPU fill**, GPU-side `card_fill`, **binding direct buffer copy**. This is a credible missed opportunity but **not yet a demonstrated Run16 wait cause**.
2. [Defeat `host.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) has `late_blocked`, `blocked_by`, `fill_ahead`, `card_fills_now`, and `VICTORY_FILLS_PLAIN` (fast tiny reads). **`VICTORY_CARD_FILLS` is OFF by default**; [Defeat README earlier same-machine trial](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/README.md) reported **worse** with it (**+11ms usual frame and +6ms GPU**), so don't rerun a toggle as if untried, nor compare its mechanism to Kelvaroq's direct CBV route as if identical.
3. [Defeat `launch.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/launch.rs) documents `VICTORY_MESH_LAUNCH` ON by default with ~5ms GPU improvement in its on/off split; [`host.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) enables `VICTORY_CARD_HASH_WIDE` by default, has async submit and card compare. **No duplicate port assigned.**
4. [Defeat `queue.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/queue.rs) already supports `VICTORY_ASYNC_PRESENT=1` or `alt` with one-frame-ahead safety constraints; [Defeat README](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/README.md) records an earlier same-owner attempt showing **no gain** while GPU limited. It is **off by default**, not a novel feature, and should only be retested if a new captured dependency shows present itself blocks command recording.
5. [Defeat `placement.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/placement.rs) already measures host GPU buffer placement without migrating; full guest-memory-in-GPU is **not presently established** on Defeat. The 12 GiB RTX4070 has limited DXGI headroom in R67 (sampled proxy low ~352MiB). Microsoft's [D3D12 heap types](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/d3d12/ne-d3d12-d3d12_heap_type), [Resizable BAR support](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/display/resizable-bar-support) and [DXGI memory budgets](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/dxgi1_4/ns-dxgi1_4-dxgi_query_video_memory_info) show CPU-visible GPU allocation / GPU local budget need actual card/driver support. **GPU_UPLOAD/ReBAR is not unlimited coherent console UMA**; CPU reads from write-combined upload-like heaps can be expensive. No blanket 2GiB ReBAR budget or full 12GiB placement on this card.
6. New Defeat commit [`080a239`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/commit/080a239bcabf77541b0af0bde555cf0ee8156a9c) after R68 **only changed README (Run16 summary)**; no new direct-constants functionality or game FPS benchmark should be inferred from that documentation commit.

#### B. Bounded FPS-first tasks for Claude and Gemini (not source-edit authorizations)

- [ ] **R69/P0-A — CLAUDE, highest ROI: measure and classify each descriptor-table constant fill.** Offline: walk Defeat `draw.rs`/ `host.rs` `Step::Fill`/ `fill_ahead`/ `blocked_by`, count **table CBVs** by (a) already addressable GPU copy **and 256B aligned within a live shadow**, (b) copy created into scratch, (c) flight writes pending and waited, (d) unaligned or mapping boundary, (e) source changed before submit. In one short separately instrumented heavy-scene capture, attach per-case **bytes, calls, aggregate CPU time, actual submit-thread wall waiting, GPU queue idle while waiting**. Compare to Kelvaroq's [patch 0038](https://github.com/stellarr-studio/kelvaroq/blob/main/native/patches/0038-direct-constants.patch) *semantics*, not toggle names. **Done when:** >=5ms/frame or a true tail-stall source is demonstrated on matched heavy windows and a safe subset/negative cases documented. If the observed cost is tiny, **close with no port**.
- [ ] **R69/P0-B — CLAUDE, safe feasibility check of direct GPU CBV binding.** Only after P0-A supports it: design a **synthetic opt-in prototype** or tests entirely under Claude ownership for CBV size/256-byte alignment, tail past shadow, in-flight GPU write→read ordering, CPU overwrite between recording/submission, two queues, page alias/remap/unmap, null/unresident physical page and descriptor rewrite. Explicitly ensure refresh is ordered **before the draw**, not silently at wrong moment. Compare exact bytes/lighting and submit stalls against current scratch/own-card-fill implementation; **do not enable a new default or claim Kelvaroq numbers**. **Done when:** byte-equal, same-image synthetic and owner A/B p50/p90/p95 and zero new dropped draws/device losses, with a reproducible **>=5ms native wall-frame win** or verified tail reduction.
- [ ] **R69/P0-C — CLAUDE: determine whether late GPU writes are blocking all fills globally.** Defeat `late_blocked` and `fill_ahead` currently avoid some waiting; show per-flight changed guest 64KiB blocks and queued consumer ranges, count false stalls, and ensure published GPU data reflects strict guest ordering. Review Kelvaroq's **narrow per-block** observation from `documentation.md`. **Done when:** the exact source of worst waits is named, along with a correctness-safe alternative and a reject case. No speculative skipped readbacks or relaxed guest status FIFO.
- [ ] **R69/P1-D — CLAUDE + GEMINI: separated UMA observability model, not blind port.** Investigate only the **individual principles** exposed by Kelvaroq's `0063-uma.patch`: immutable or generation-stamped physical-page backing, CPU-visible vs GPU-owned bytes, GPU VA pool binds, last-producer fence, descriptor on-write bookkeeping and aliases. First construct a **non-mutating ownership/copy traffic census** on Defeat (can reuse `VICTORY_PLACEMENT_LOOK`, `VICTORY_GPU_ACCESS_COSTS`, `VICTORY_READBACK_REUSE` observations) for the owner RTX4070. Measure range count, owner transitions, exact CPU read-back consumers, actual changed bytes and per-page mapping churn. **Done when:** a realistic 12GB budgeted page/alias state machine and WARP concurrency tests say whether **any bounded subset** can save >5ms without lost writes/texture popping. No direct-memory migration until that evidence exists.
- [ ] **R69/P1-E — CLAUDE benchmark discipline/possible opt-in already present:** do not redo existing `MESH_LAUNCH`, `CARD_HASH_WIDE`, `COPY_PLAIN` or `CARD_FILLS` trial. `ASYNC_PRESENT` was already tested with **no clear gain** in earlier Defeat README; only re-open if Run17 shows present serialization specifically now drives p90. Keep diagnostic timeline separate from FPS. **Done when:** each candidate has unique current source behavior, nonduplicate implementation, minimal local test and same-scene native-FPS p50/p90 with the original visually intact.
- [ ] **R69/P2-F — GEMINI primary-source proof packet:** independently compare **Kelvaroq `0038` direct CBV** to Defeat `card_fill` and general scratch fill, including memory barriers and D3D12 CBV alignment; test claim that `fill_ahead` global waits apply to Defeat's exact current code; examine `0063` patch for actual per-page fence/alias ownership and whether promised direct-VRAM remains an unshipped experiment. Cross-check [Microsoft D3D12 upload heap](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/d3d12/ne-d3d12-d3d12_heap_type), [residency](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/direct3d12/residency), [IDXGI budget](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/dxgi1_4/ns-dxgi1_4-dxgi_query_video_memory_info) and [Resizable BAR](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/display/resizable-bar-support) against a 12GB GeForce. For every technique: exact source at commit, stage-specific metrics, counterexample where wrong, WARP/RTX4070 bounded experiment, **stop condition**, license/attribution. Do not propose copying private engine code.
- [ ] **R69/P2-G — RESEARCH/OWNER integration:** preserve R67/Run16 as baseline. When Claude supplies per-fill and tail-latency data, rank the top one or two concrete changes. Compare native rendered fps versus generated fps, same save/route/warm cache, scene lighting/hair/skin and first-visible texture, and Windows memory usage. Mark a checkbox only with actual evidence. **Done when:** test receipt names source SHA, log switches, sample N, p50/p90, scene/camera and visuals, and it informs exactly one next A/B.

**Net disposition:** This Gemini report contributes **one especially useful new Defeat-specific investigation—direct binding of general descriptor constants to their ordered GPU backing copy**—plus an experimental full-memory architecture to study cautiously. Do not reinterpret public/experimental Kelvaroq GPU-floor and offline microbenchmark figures as delivered gameplay FPS, and do not send Claude tasks to reimplement already working launches/hashes/copy/async present. **No emulator source changes authorized or made by this research entry.**


### R70 — DLSS Super Resolution and Frame Generation: multi-vendor feasibility (2026-11-28; research-only)

> **Owner request:** Research **DLSS, frame generation and similar enhancements** for Defeat on owner's **GeForce RTX 4070 12GB + Ryzen 7 5800X**; record priority tasks for Claude and Gemini **without editing emulator code**. Inputs: current GitHub repos, primary vendor programming guides, Defeat Run16 R67 context; **no owner trial or screenshot, no new Gemini FG report attachment available to reconcile yet**. Category: FEATURE investigation and **display FPS**, separately from core native-FPS R67–R69. Checkboxes stay open until tested. **Never treat 2x generated scanouts as 2x emulated frames**.

**Actual source examined (NVIDIA fork HEAD a4d05e7):** [full integration guide](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/docs/dlss.md), [Streamline D3D12 adapter](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/crates/victory-gpu/src/streamline.rs), [manual ABI definitions](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/crates/victory-gpu/src/streamline/abi.rs), [render scale/flags/camera](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/crates/victory-gpu/src/streamline/plan.rs), [FSR3 frame generation backend](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/crates/victory-gpu/src/streamline/fsr.rs), [Xbox FidelityFX HLE interposer](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/crates/victory-hle/src/modules/fidelityfx.rs), [Series X retail v1.0.2.1 render-path selection](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/a4d05e7fd0af904130809c334a6008d62c24db6a/apps/victory/src/native_1440p.rs). [fxceless/victory-fork](https://github.com/fxceless/victory-fork/tree/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/streamline) and [foxhound](https://github.com/readmet3xt-lab/foxhound/tree/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/streamline) also contain predecessor Streamline pieces. [DexNom upscaler/host DLL bridge](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/crates/victory-hle/src/modules/fidelityfx.rs) and [FSR quality modes](https://github.com/DexNom/VIctory/blob/main/apps/victory/src/fsr_preset.rs) are further references. **Kelvaroq's README explicitly lists DLSS/FG as NOT implemented public milestones**, so do not cite its GPU-floor experiment as FG evidence.

**Vendor primary documentation (checked Nov 2026):** [NVIDIA Streamline generic guide](https://github.com/NVIDIA-RTX/Streamline/blob/main/docs/ProgrammingGuide.md), [DLSS-SR](https://github.com/NVIDIA-RTX/Streamline/blob/main/docs/ProgrammingGuideDLSS.md), [DLSS-G](https://github.com/NVIDIA-RTX/Streamline/blob/main/docs/ProgrammingGuideDLSS_G.md), [Reflex](https://github.com/NVIDIA-RTX/Streamline/blob/main/docs/ProgrammingGuideReflex.md), [Streamline releases](https://github.com/NVIDIA-RTX/Streamline/releases), [AMD FSR SDK frame generation swap chain](https://gpuopen.com/manuals/fsr_sdk/techniques/frame-interpolation-swap-chain/), [AMD FSR SDK source](https://github.com/GPUOpen-LibrariesAndSDKs/FidelityFX-SDK), [Intel XeSS 3 SDK](https://github.com/intel/xess), [Intel XeSS-FG guide](https://www.intel.com/content/www/us/en/developer/articles/technical/xess-fg-developer-guide.html), [Intel supported-hardware table](https://www.intel.com/content/www/us/en/developer/topic-technology/gamedev/xess.html). The fork's tested **Streamline SDK 2.14.16** is a **pin of that project**, not a claim it is the newest/current correct version; source ABI/layouts must be checked against actual SDK headers before building.

#### A. Native FPS, displayed FPS, quality and applicability

| Technology | RTX4070 feasibility | Why / expected type of benefit | Place in roadmap |
|---|---|---|---|
| **DLSS Super Resolution (SR)** | **Yes**, RTX-series NVIDIA SDK/device supported | Lower *actual internal render resolution* and reconstruct full-resolution scene from **color, depth, dense motion, jitter and temporal history**. Can improve native FPS **only to the extent GPU shading/rendering dominates**. Defeat Run16 queue is often empty while CPU submits; no guaranteed FPS gain. | **First graphics integration after resource audit** |
| **DLAA** | Yes, DLSS native-resolution mode | Better temporal AA/shimmer; typically quality mode, **not faster native FPS**. | P2 optional |
| **DLSS Frame Generation (DLSS-G)** | **RTX40 2x official** if driver, HAGS, OS and SDK support queries pass. NVIDIA RTX50 Multi Frame Generation 3x–6x is **not an official RTX4070 promise**. | Interpolates additional **displayed** frames between game-rendered frames; does **not speed title logic, input update or the 168ms native Run16 frame**, and adds VRAM/latency/graphics cost. At native ~6 fps, ideal 2x is ~12 displayed fps before drop/overhead; feature may look worse than native due artifacts. | Opt-in **experiment after DLSS SR**, not core FPS substitute |
| **NVIDIA Reflex** | Supported RTX; required counterpart for NVIDIA FG integration | Supplies consistent per-frame simulation/render/present markers/latency mode to control GPU/render queues; not a generic 100ms CPU blocker fix. | Paired with DLSS-G |
| **AMD FSR 3.x Frame Generation** | Cross-vendor DX12 in suitable SDK version, includes RTX-class devices | AMD FidelityFX **frame interpolation swapchain** with UI and motion/depth inputs; separate proxy swapchain. AMD docs warn visually poor output below 60 native fps for FSR3 FG; at Run16 ~6 native fps this is a high-risk demonstration. Do not confuse with newer AMD-driver-only FSR4 FG support for RDNA4. | P2 cross-vendor fallback |
| **AMD FidelityFX / FSR temporal upscaling** | General DX12 alternative | May help GPU-bound frames if lower real render scale; DexNom host FFX DLL provider handles only **upscaler**, refuses other effects. Do not assume the console's shipped Series S DLL supports arbitrary Series X size. | P2 alternative |
| **Intel XeSS 3 SR + FG** | **Non-Intel GPU SM6.4+ class supported by vendor for ordinary FG**, subject to runtime query; Intel XeLL required. XeSS **Multi** FG limited by Intel hardware table | Alternative for non-NVIDIA devices or visual-quality comparison; own backend and swapchain, **no simultaneous vendor FG**. | P2/P3 after one robust frame-path abstraction |
| **DLSS Ray Reconstruction** | RTX capable but *scene is not yet eligible* | Expects true RT noisy signals / motion, normals, roughness and reliable radiance; Defeat BVH builder OFF/Wave64 missing. No source-grounded method to invent correct rays through RR. | **Defer** until rays/rendering verified |
| **Dynamic resolution and sharpen** | Renderer-specific | Dynamic internal scaling needs history invalidation, UI and render-target relayout; sharpening can look better but not replace output correctly. | P2 after fixed-size path |

**Crucial arithmetic:** native ~6 fps = ~167ms per game frame; 2x FG can at most *aim* for ~12 scanouts/sec from those frames, less if dropped. It does **not** become 30 or 60 native fps, and low-frame-rate interpolation produces larger motion prediction gaps and may worsen input response. Keep on-screen counters **native present, interpolated shown, dropped/late and latency**, not one ambiguous “FPS”. Keep Ray Tracing OFF during initial FPS testing unless intentionally comparing visual modes.

#### B. How VIctory-NVIDIA actually wires it — and Defeat's present gaps

1. **Host upscaler discovery / renderer selection is a prerequisite.** [NVIDIA fork guide](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/docs/dlss.md) says **stock Series X renderer 6 uses title's own temporal pass**, while **Series S renderer 7** invokes Xbox FidelityFX via `amd_fidelityfx_loader_xbox.dll`. Their `native_1440p.rs` *opt-in* retail-image patch for build **1.0.2.1** selects the FFX path and reconciles render size, internal scene scale and output without assuming FFX query alone controls geometry. Each expected original code byte/hash is checked: mismatched build => no patch. Defeat [`apps/victory/src/run.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/apps/victory/src/run.rs) already loads title DLLs, but source audit found **no equivalent Series X upscaler selection patch**. Claude must independently validate the actual title image version, with failure **falling back to unmodified original**, no unknown-build binary patch.
2. **Only intercept the upscaler dispatch, not unrelated Xbox API.** NVIDIA-fork [FidelityFX HLE bridge](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-hle/src/modules/fidelityfx.rs) wraps imports `ffxCreateContext`, `ffxDestroyContext`, `ffxConfigure`, `ffxQuery`, `ffxDispatch`, keeps **original context/dispatch fallback**, and takes only upscaling dispatch `0x10001` when conditions pass. [Defeat `crates/victory-hle/src/modules/mod.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-hle/src/modules/mod.rs) currently **does not contain/declare a `fidelityfx` module** and [`HleResolver`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-hle/src/lib.rs) has host/module/missing policy. Upstream source code not “one switch to enable” here. Note source license and title-specific hashes.
3. **Execute in the original guest command-list order.** On matching FFX dispatch, NVIDIA's GPU bridge reuses **scene color, depth, motion vectors, exposure, reactive/transparency masks, output**, output/render extents, correct temporal jitter, motion scale, inverted-Z and reset flag; translates Xbox D3D12 guest resource states, flushes/refreshes physical aliases where needed, and records `slEvaluateFeature` at the same logical point as the native upscaler. It marks output GPU-written and restores subsequent draw pipeline state. No extra synthetic frames should be passed to guest CPU/render stage.
4. **Correct GPU proxy lifetime.** [NVIDIA fork `streamline.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/streamline.rs) uses **selected Streamline proxies** (`slUpgradeInterface`) and explicit device handoff, *not global D3D12 detours*. Defeat's actual anchor points are [`host.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs): host D3D12 device/queue creation around **1417–1475**, `Presenter` around **11053**, actual `Present` around **11574**, swapchain creation around **11616**; [`queue.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/queue.rs) includes async/ordered present. Device/queue proxy, frame token, DXGI factory and FFX resource must share **correct lifetime, queue/fence generation and reset**; never double-present a generated frame through the guest.
5. **Build HUD-free scene and valid frame-indexed motion input for FG.** NVIDIA's title-specific [DLSS documentation](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/docs/dlss.md) recognizes a particular full-screen UI composite shader and **copies the rendered scene before UI blending** for its `kBufferTypeHUDLessColor`. This is a heuristic tied to a renderer/title version; the same repo warns *other UI writes can precede it*, affecting HUD warp. Prefer explicit UI alpha/color if available; screenshot same-camera menus/subtitles/reticle/hair/poles and animated characters. Match near/far depth inversion, MV sign/scaling (render vs display pixels), per-frame jitter, camera matrices and reset. **A plausible looking synthetic optical flow alone is insufficient for frame gen correctness.**
6. **No FG without a new correctly submitted upscaler.** NVIDIA's Streamline records depth/MV at the **last submitted** upscale before a present, tags frame 0 viewport for FG and viewport 1 for SR, sends NVIDIA Reflex markers, and disables FG for loads/menus/resize/missing inputs; DLSS-only mode can use its own present proxy for plugin reclamation. [NVIDIA vendor guide](https://github.com/NVIDIA-RTX/Streamline/blob/main/docs/ProgrammingGuideDLSS_G.md) requires compatible HAGS and tagged depth/dense motion/HUD-less color, explicit FG enable and correct frame-token semantics. Defeat may present asynchronously; prove **frame index, last submitted upscale, and resource states** match the target present, rather than relying on the latest recorded texture.
7. **Safe official SDK distribution / fallback.** NVIDIA fork loads **NVIDIA-signed** Streamline and NGX runtime DLLs from a configured SDK folder (example `VICTORY_STREAMLINE_DIR` and tested 2.14.16), checks signatures/feature support, and retains title-upscaler/plain-present fallback. Official SDK binaries are **not checked into the repo**; users source them under official NVIDIA licensing, with a trusted loader path and clean shutdown. Do not use unsigned/third-party FG “unlockers” as a required dependency or rewrite title's binary without owner approval. The source's `victory upscaler-check` **offline** mode tests plugin load, GPU feature caps, stand-in colors/motion/depth and present output; replicate the **test design**, not necessarily the exact CLI.

**12GB VRAM safety:** Defeat Run16 R67 minimum sampled local-budget headroom proxy was **~352MiB**, while NVIDIA fork reports about **489MiB FG incremental used memory at 1440p in one scoped observation**, and foxhound elsewhere says ~1.4GiB broadly. Different states/measurements; no fixed 20% guess guarantees safety. If foreground GPU pool/FSR/DLSS features require e.g. >headroom, **leave disabled with a clear error**, proactively reduce texture residency only after existing Defeat [budget/texture room](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) gates and avoid forced shared-memory overcommit. DXGI budget must be rechecked *just before* creating SDK resources and during frame generation; track process `CurrentUsage/Budget`, copy queue stalls, texture blackness and fallback on OOM/device removal. No hard-coded “4096 buffers” or new VRAM allocations to improve mere counters.

#### C. Claude + Gemini prioritized research tasks and acceptance tests

- [ ] **R70/P0-A CLAUDE: prove existing upscaler/FX API dispatch availability and runtime output sizes.** On a normal owner build, record (anonymized) whether Series X/Series S loads FFX, whether upscaler dispatch `0x10001` occurs, actual render/output size, color/depth/MV formats and states, already-available HUD/scene split, frame ID and timestamp. Check `victory census` and `apps/victory/src/run.rs` loader paths. Gate: **one full original upscaler dispatch receipt** or clear version-safe explanation why it is not used, with no fps/behavior changes.
- [ ] **R70/P0-B CLAUDE + GEMINI: architecture/SDK matrix, no runtime source changes yet.** Map title image stock build vs Defeat source; specify HLE upscaler interception and host `external` ordered command-list hook; compare NVIDIA `fidelityfx.rs`, DexNom host FFX bridge, and Streamline `plan.rs` against actual fields. Supply exact SDK-supported GPU/Windows/HAGS feature queries and immutable DLL signature verification. Gate: full **dataflow/lifetime graph**, off-by-default feature switches, licensing and fail-closed version checks, no title-code-unknown patch.
- [ ] **R70/P1-C CLAUDE: pure offline synthetic SDK harness (first executable milestone).** Inspired by upstream `upscaler-check`: create D3D12 device/queue/swapchain and mocked owned color/depth/MV/output textures, verify installed vendor DLL versions, correctness of API structs & command-list state, one upscale and clean resource cleanup/shutdown on RTX4070, then turn *everything off* and verify identical normal startup. Gate: **100 cycles of create/evaluate/resize/destroy** without warnings, leaks, DXGI error, driver removal; no game assets required.
- [ ] **R70/P1-D CLAUDE: optional DLSS SR only** after P0 contract, P1 harness. Test Quality/Balanced/Performance/ DLAA **one at a time**, exact requested internal resolution, no new dropped draws, no stale texture uploads. Compare same warm-cache/camera scene, screenshot hair/subtitles/edges, native p50/p90 and GPU time and GPU queue idle, VRAM budget. **Stop** if GPU idle dominates or real frame time/fidelity worsens; do not report SDK output without title-produced frames.
- [ ] **R70/P1-E CLAUDE: optional NVIDIA DLSS FG 2× + Reflex with HUDless safety**, only after SR/resolution path and owner approval. Prove each present corresponds to submitted upscale, depth and MV history, timestamp/viewport token, Reflex marker, UI-free scene. Exercise camera yaw, scene cuts, pause/map, subtitles, water, resize, fullscreen and device-lost fallback; verify **rendered vs generated vs displayed** via in-app counters and [Intel PresentMon](https://github.com/GameTechDev/PresentMon) as independent observer. **Stop** for visible warp/input lag/VRAM eviction/OOM, missing HAGS support, wrong UI tagging, >5% native p90 regression or unstable resource lifetime; default OFF.
- [ ] **R70/P2-F CLAUDE: AMD FSR3 FG backend and existing FFX upscaler fallback.** Use [AMD FidelityFX SDK 3.1.7 swapchain documentation](https://gpuopen.com/manuals/fsr_sdk/techniques/frame-interpolation-swap-chain/) and NVIDIA-fork `streamline/fsr.rs`; separately implement capability gate for FSR3 and NVIDIA (never both at once); check correct `IDXGISwapChain4` compatible resource lifetime / UI callbacks and no framegen when no new upscale. Gate: **same synthetic harness + scene parity + retained baseline**; reject any fallback actually invoking Series S-only internal resolution when Series X format differs.
- [ ] **R70/P2-G GEMINI / separate: XeSS 3 SR/FG and latency alternatives.** Read Intel's latest XeSS SDK, required XeLL component, Intel/non-Intel feature caps and **separate XeSS proxy swapchain**, and identify a reusable provider interface and detection/fallback contract rather than 3 independent simultaneous interceptors. Gate: primary-source API table, tested supported/non-supported GPU paths and no unsupported promise of RTX40 “Multi-FG 6×.” AMD/Intel vendor defaults must be rechecked for current releases.
- [ ] **R70/P2-H OWNER + CLAUDE: compare what user actually perceives.** Preserve **Run16 native FPS baseline**, record native rendered frame p50/p90/p99, real game input latency, SDK/interpolation overhead, actual screen present rate, cutscene/menus/streaming correctness, and DXGI budget. Try lower internal 1440p→output mode only if actual title output/input artifacts stay correct. Gate: named same save/route, real sample N, tested off/Quality/FG combinations, minimum-budget margin, screenshot parity; no “30fps emulated” from a 6-native/12-displayed test.
- [ ] **R70/P3-I deferred: Ray Reconstruction and vendor-specific MFG/driver mods.** Don't spend FPS research time on RR until builder/ray samples are correct. No required unsigned unlocker, mystery driver override, or untested “instant DLSS” DLL swap; user's actual 4070 official support is **2× Frame Generation**.

**Reporting language for Claude:** “DLSS Super Resolution potentially lowers **GPU cost** at reduced internal resolution; DLSS/FSR/XeSS Frame Generation increases **displayed frames**, contingent on real-frame cadence, optical-flow correctness, latency and VRAM. Current Run16 is heavily CPU/synchronization constrained (~168ms p50 in >=3000-draw windows), so maintain the native-FPS optimization lane as P0. No performance gains for Defeat have yet been measured for these features.”


### R71 — VIctory-NVIDIA / foxhound source audit: why ~20 native rendered FPS without Frame Generation (2026-11-28; research-only)

> **Question:** What *specifically* makes VIctory-NVIDIA ~20fps without FG, and what can Defeat use safely on **RTX4070 12GB / Ryzen5800X**? Sources inspected to exact fork commit `a4d05e7`, foxhound `3d52ac8`, Defeat `main` and owner R67/Run16. A **36KB detailed TXT Claude handoff** accompanies this checklist update (delivered in current chat). This is **NOT** a request for Claude to cherry-pick a renderer or change emulator code automatically. No new owner A/B run occurred.

**Reported base FPS, carefully scoped:** VIctory-NVIDIA [README](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/README.md) states own **26–30 actual rendered fps (33–38ms)** on **RTX5070/9800X3D**, Series X 2560×1440 output, **DLSS Quality**, **RT OFF**, opening rooms; FG adds displayed scanouts. Prior build 15–17fps in same rooms but **Series S 720→1080 DLSS**, before foxhound base rebase, so not isolated causal A/B. Author's "**memory compares moved to card: 11→~22fps**" is an earlier intermediate configuration claim lacking matched public raw logs on owner's machine. Independent [same-save warm community run](https://www.reddit.com/r/FitGirlRepack/comments/1y71rcu/update_on_my_findings_so_far_nvidia/) saw **17.8 actual rendered fps**, **DLSS Performance + 2× FG**, separately counted; differing hardware. [Foxhound](https://github.com/readmet3xt-lab/foxhound/blob/main/README.md) RTX4070 SUPER/7800X3D 1440p Series X **DLSS Quality and FG OFF** reached 27–30fps opening and ~21fps first water; its **newer Nov28** 20min drive p50 **33.3ms**, mean **34.4ms** uses newer code **not proven merged** into NVIDIA `a4d05e7`. **Neither fork substantiates a same-scene 20fps benchmark with BOTH DLSS SR and FG disabled.** Also explicit visual tradeoffs: RT builder OFF, screen-space ambient/reflection approximation, optional omitted shader passes.

**Evidence-based sources and exact differentials**

| Rank | Fork mechanism with code anchor | Defeat equivalence / hypothesis | Gate before implementing |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P0** | NVIDIA GPU card copies/64KiB hashes [shadow.rs](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) + [host.rs `CARD_COMPARE`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs). RTX4070 SUPER author code comment: 512→2048MiB GPU-buffer cap, ~592→1979MiB local copies, costly compute passes 42/32/27ms→0.19/0.25/0.41ms, source claims **21–25fps** in a particular run | **Already has GPU compares**, [Defeat `shadow.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) default **1024MiB cap, min1MiB**; NVIDIA budget-tuned <=12GiB default **18% max2048MiB, min16MiB**. Defeat Run16 only ~352MiB min sampled free budget, ~11.3GB device use. **No 2GB auto switch!** | Safe 512/768/1024MiB cap × 1/16MiB min A/B; log residency/bytes, DXGI Budget/CurrentUsage, copyback, GPU busy/empty, black textures, native p50/p90. Higher cap only with proven reserve |
| **P0** | [NVIDIA `list/thin.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/list/thin.rs) `VICTORY_THIN=1` writes guest call arguments into chunks and worker-translates; source records 45k–60k calls/frame, 160–190ms summed work across threads and 55/65ms frame record stage in old scene | **Not in Defeat main**. Async submit/QUEUE2 are different; Run16 GPU empty 85.35ms median, 74.1ms while submitter busy **not necessarily guest recorder** | Profile guest call count/total thread time and actual critical-path blocking; only if >10ms critical work pilot opt-in thin subset preserving pointer snapshot, guest order/fences and reset |
| **P1** | NVIDIA [descriptor host `spans_quick`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/descriptor/host.rs) bypasses `placed` lock when slot cannot affect placed views; source comments point to ~4/16ms thread wait in past run. `VICTORY_SYNC_IN_PLACE`, `VICTORY_HEAP_SYNC_AT_SUBMIT`, `TABLED_REREAD` present in foxhound defaults | Defeat has advanced descriptor levels/slot tracking but no named `SPANS_QUICK` path; equivalence **needs exact functions** | Measure `placed` lock waits, slot rewrites, heap sync time, dropped/stale views, image parity; >=5ms real wall-frame win before port |
| **P1** | `VICTORY_SOFT_VIEWS=copies` keeps tiled page mapping in guest-runtime books without Windows view remap churn; `VICTORY_PRELUDE_POOL` keeps shared D3D12 small list allocators (~1100→190 alive author claim) | Defeat texture-no-wait remap and various pools may overlap, but not identical source | Count remap/allocator wait p90 first; test guest CPU alias coherence, page zeros, memory releases and crash rates |
| **P1** | [Foxhound README](https://github.com/readmet3xt-lab/foxhound/blob/main/README.md) `victory shaders`, `victory warm`, pipeline book and deterministic static sampler order prevent cold compile and repeated stalls | Defeat has shader/PSO cache; compare path+key and actual cache hit on **same build** | Compare *warm* runs only and track hidden compile stalls; no first-run vs second-run speed credit |
| **P2 / hazardous** | NVIDIA [`WC_NO_BACK`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/shadow.rs) skips CPU copyback of large write-combined guest ranges, measured ~360/730MiB declared there; when applied to all sizes it **made cutscenes almost black** from stale exposure | Defeat `VICTORY_READBACK_REUSE` records copies, **not actual guest CPU reads**. Unsafe blind skip violates copyback/alias/physics correctness | Research-only classification with guest CPU read tracing, page-lifetime/alias tracking, real fences, in-image parity; no skip without proof |
| **P2 / fidelity choice** | [Ambient stand-in](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/ambient.rs) and `VICTORY_LEAVE_OUT` skip RT/heathaze/decal defrag; NVIDIA `VICTORY_WATER_LIGHT=0` eliminates two buggy water shaders author says ~15ms GPU, water becomes darker | Defeat BVH builder OFF Run16 but different visual quality; skip ≠ equivalent emulation. RT direct effect can't be called 'without anything'. | Separate optional **approximate graphics mode** from fidelity tests; benchmark only with marked quality changes, never misreport as same-work fps |

**Explicit non-leads:** NVIDIA [`host/upcheck.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host/upcheck.rs) is expensive *diagnostics* OFF default, **not GPU-compare speed code**; [`host/card_steps.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host/card_steps.rs) says `VICTORY_CARD_STEPS` **OFF default**, not proved on in advertised 20fps, related to R69 selective direct constants; [`host/two_queues.rs`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/blob/main/crates/victory-gpu/src/host/two_queues.rs) says `VICTORY_ASYNC_QUEUE=plan` **plans** a second queue but currently submits on one. Foxhound `VICTORY_PORT_CONSTANTS` OFF on 12GB because less CPU can mean more GPU stalls. Do not count FG, DLSS or no-RT picture as an isolated real-FPS emulator win.

> **Inputs:** owner-uploaded `run17.zip` (`run17.txt`, 37,968 lines), `gpu17.csv` (UTF-16, 785 samples), and pasted ~810-row NVIDIA telemetry CSV (overlaps `gpu17.csv`). **Performance, correct metric:** percentile of the *5–6-second log REPORT WINDOW AVERAGES*, not per-individual-frame p90. For >=3k draws/window: Run16 **N60 p50 168.0ms / p90 336.5ms** (~6.0fps inverse p50), Run17 **N57 p50 169.6ms / p90 280.0ms** (~5.90fps), with median draws **6034.5 vs 5387.0**. No causal native-FPS improvement proven. Run17 3k+ prep p50 **90.8ms**, CPU title copies p50 **42.9ms**, relay copyback p50 **40.7ms**; *overlapping* costs. Run17 all per-second card-clock reports p50 **70.8ms GPU queue empty**, **59.8ms empty while submitter busy**; in broad heavy-window time span ~89.1ms / 78.4ms. Not a GPU-shader-bound workload. Source log's `0.0 on the GPU` frame-line label is not measured total GPU busy time.

**Major confirmed defects and constraints:**

DXGI worst sampled local budget slack **298MB** (Run16 **352MB**); device-wide NVIDIA used max **11,886MiB**. | Residency and missing/late texture risk. Categories not additive; the sampling and scenes differ. **No blind 2GB buffer allocation / FG**. Source `shadow.rs` normal min1MiB/cap1024MiB but Run17 card-copy report shows **98 copies / 2086MB**: reconcile accounting/cap meaning before tuning. |
| **P0 correctness** | **26 host D3D12 compute pipelines refused**, all `wave_size=64`, `0x80070057`; **26** paired Wave64-translator fallback messages, including water `5dc3b25986622949` and `6f225b641c5b0708`. **29** separate unsupported pipeline creation method warnings. | Missing shader work and potential water/color wrongness. ### R74 — Run18: verified DirectStorage retained-view smoothness gain, remaining guest/texture stalls (2026-11-28; research-only)

> **Owner observation confirmed by logs:** Run18 **felt noticeably smoother**. Checked original `run18.zip` (`run18.txt`, ~37,705 lines), `gpu18.csv` (756 device-wide samples), plus reopened Run17 and Run16. Source label Run18 **`174e3a411c6a`**, Run17 **`0dc0c3dd01eb`**. [Compare](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/compare/0dc0c3dd01eb6158d80a19bab40e1dc66ce6115c...174e3a411c6a793ccadf5afa2703bfbbcd44bcce) identifies **only one runtime code change**, Claude commit [`88109d7`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/commit/88109d7bd17c17ade512af383544ffab2fd5ccd0) in `xmem.rs` + `dstorage.rs`: `VICTORY_DSTORAGE_KEEP_VIEWS` ON by default, `=0` restores per-write mapping. The two other commits are docs-only. Run17 had `VICTORY_TITLE_CALLS=1`, Run18 did not; same owner RTX4070/5800X and builder OFF; **different scene/draw mix**. A **more detailed 14.5KB TXT for Claude** accompanies this checklist in the chat: `Defeat_R74_Claude_Detailed_Run18_KeptViews_NativeFPS_2026-11-28.txt`.

**Strongest result — real streaming responsiveness:** summed per-5s DStorage interval counters Run17 751s: **204 requests >1s**, **2,866 >250ms**, max **2,265ms**; Run18 698s: **ZERO >1s**, **182 >250ms**, max **699ms** (~94% fewer >250ms; per-minute ~228.9→15.6). Run18 logs **231,380 writes through kept physical-page views, ZERO made-per-write views** over 670.7s of new view reports. The intended map/commit/unmap-per-request overhead appears eliminated; a real code change and aligned owner observation, although scene traffic not matched.

**Native FPS context:** logs report 5–6s interval *average* frame times, and percentiles here are of those **window averages**, not individual-frame p90. For windows with **>=3,000 average draws**: Run16 N60 p50 **168.0ms**, Run17 N57 **169.6ms**, Run18 N73 **149.5ms** (~**5.9→6.7 inverse-median rendered FPS** vs Run17); **Run18 p90 309.3ms vs Run17 280.0ms**, so severe tail not fixed. **Median draws 5,387→4,639**, confounding inference. By draw band p50: **3k–4.6k 180.2→170.3ms (N22→34)**, **4.6k–6.9k 157.2→141.7ms (N27→34)**, **6.9k–9k 170.7→149.5ms (N8→5)**. No same-save ON/OFF test, so *causal native-FPS gain is promising, not proven*. Heavy-window prep p50 **90.8→79.5ms**, copyback **40.7→38.9ms** (overlapping thread costs). Heavy-period per-second GPU-queue idle p50 **89.1→76.2ms**, of that while submitting **78.4→68.1ms**, still CPU/guest side.

**New P0 bottleneck observed, not yet causal:** [`xmem::physical()`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/88109d7bd17c17ade512af383544ffab2fd5ccd0/crates/victory-hle/src/modules/xmem.rs) now times the `PHYSICAL` page-table mutex. Run18 reports **537,621ms cumulative wait across ALL threads** over 670.7s, with a **36,099ms aggregate wait in one ~5.4s interval**. **Never mistake aggregated concurrent-thread waits for frame-wall time.** Need thread-ID/lock-holder/guest-frame dependency; `watch.rs` guarded alias updates and DirectStorage require atomic mappings. Run18 residual **03:20:43 ~1,722ms / 4,023 draws**, GPU idle ~1,613ms; **03:28:19 ~3,330ms / 5 draws (transition)**, GPU idle ~3,278ms; **03:28:27 ~3,561ms / 3,698 draws**, GPU idle ~3,452ms, texture work ~562ms and 2,933MB reported; **03:28:43 953ms / 6,048 draws**, GPU idle ~852ms. Individual DStorage requests nearby stay below 700ms; **do not attribute those stalls to DStorage's individual-request duration or GPU compute** without a cross-thread/fence trace.

**Other Run18 issues do NOT vanish:** sampled warning `a texture picked by index changed after its upload with nothing to tell` logs **327 lines**, actual cumulative counter at least **127,001** (Run17 **109,001**), *not* a matched-rate regression; **49,000** cumulative already-resident refreshes while no room for *new* textures (Run17 **13,000**), strong VRAM/residency pressure but not 49k failed allocations. Local DXGI minimum sampled headroom **225MB** vs Run17 **298MB**; GPU18 device-wide peak memory-used **11,862MiB**; keep these scopes distinct. **26 Wave64 D3D12 compute pipeline rejects** persist, plus existing shader-visible view heap cap 1M vs guest 1.245M. No logged device removal or OOM. R72 image correctness tasks retain priority.

**New high-priority long-session correctness finding: page number exhaustion and truncation (research-only; not observed reached).** [Defeat `xmem.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/e08088759375b2f70ea670583228e61a19a1afbb/crates/victory-hle/src/modules/xmem.rs) `Pages.next_page` starts at `0x100000`; `XMemAllocatePhysicalPages` increments forever by `n*FRAMES` (`FRAMES=16`) and `XMemFreePhysicalPages` deletes IDs but **does not reuse**. Its source comment documents the title preserves only **21 bits of `number >> 4`**, implying `PAGE_LIMIT=1<<25`. Total lifetime physical allocations after starting offset can cross that in **2,031,616 × 64KiB = 124 GiB cumulatively**, even if freed along the way. **No evidence Runs1–18 hit threshold.** New NVIDIA commit [`108a8971`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/commit/108a897113512a5a3fea87cd63ef77e1e86aaaa1) caps and reuses lowest free contiguous run, returning failure if exhausted, plus boundary tests. Claude should audit ID encoding, active alias/fence lifetime, cumulative use and source diff BEFORE deciding a fix. This is a concrete source-level correctness gap, not an owner FPS claim.

**New real cross-fork source audit (R73 previously had README-only evidence):** accessible [Belobug-style source mirror, pinned](https://github.com/uRyumasu/victory-belobug-amd/tree/cd8dfc06ace7ccb91a19431e74bc2d0de2a10145) contains:

Defeat `Spans` may already help; only investigate if Run19 still profiles guest fault blocking >5ms wall.
- [`port_journal.rs`](https://github.com/uRyumasu/victory-belobug-amd/blob/a-publier/crates/victory-gpu/src/port_journal.rs) + [`watch.rs` shard notes](https://github.com/uRyumasu/victory-belobug-amd/blob/a-publier/crates/victory-gpu/src/watch.rs): **64 RwLock shards plus early descriptor sync rounds**, queue wait floors and prep writes. Source admits shader writes landing in heap during prepare are not covered by current ordering argument; whole-frame improvement only **1.3–2.9ms** on original AMD stack. Not P0 port.
- [`port_cbv.rs`](https://github.com/uRyumasu/victory-belobug-amd/blob/a-publier/crates/victory-gpu/src/port_cbv.rs) **depends on** [`port.rs` resident guest constants](https://github.com/uRyumasu/victory-belobug-amd/blob/a-publier/crates/victory-gpu/src/port.rs) AND shader translator raw CBV semantics (16-byte Xbox alignment vs host 256-byte CBV); not a standalone `=1` knob in Defeat. Missed patches can retain old view for multiple frames; **does not fix 1M host descriptor cap**. Requires exact build SHA+image parity/rollback.

Its author reports ~45–60k COM calls/frame and 55/65ms recorder critical path on DIFFERENT hardware. Defeat lacks THIN; instrument guest title recording vs Defeat submitter before prototype. New NVIDIA commit [`5986a64d`](https://github.com/trustno1-coder/VIctory-NVIDIA/commit/5986a64d) warns heads may disappear with THIN on RTX5080: full-picture parity gate.
