|---|---|---|---|
| xmem page-table lock waits | 537,621ms all-thread wait in 670.7s; **48,095ms/min** | 1,024ms all-thread wait in 627.5s; **98ms/min** | **~99.8% reduction in summed thread wait** across the already implemented [separate `PHYSICAL`, `PAGES`, `SHOWN` locks](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/e992203c7abe5e400d0742dc087bf8d7771bdd4b/crates/victory-hle/src/modules/xmem.rs), NOT 99.8% FPS gain |
| DirectStorage requests >250ms / >1s | 182 / 0 during 698.2s; longest 699ms | **0 / 0** during 899s; longest 249ms | Real continued streaming smoothness, scene traffic unmatched; keep mapped views and guest notices |
| 3k+ draws sparse FPS line percentile | N=73: median **149.5ms** | N=78: interpolated **148.7ms**, p90 264.0ms vs Run18 p90 309.3ms | Only a small sampled native-FPS change; **Claude's runstats lower median/rank convention gives 148.2ms, p90 275.4ms**, same records |
| 3k+ draws full ~5s Present intervals | **not logged** | **2,611 intervals in 414.2s = 6.30 presentations/s** | New and more complete time-domain evidence, not equal to scene-matched quality-adjusted FPS |
| Guards waited / held p50 across broad heavy span | 43.8 / 26.5ms aggregate all threads | **32.6 / 19.9ms** aggregate | `Spans` clean-range lookup promising; VEH still waits at site **2261** |
| Worst-sampled conservative local DXGI budget slack | **225MB** | **112MB** at **17:47:05** | Very tight 12GB GPU; no blind large VRAM allocations |

**IMPORTANT correction of all earlier R16–R75 FPS terminology:** Source [`host.rs::Meter::presented`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/e992203c7abe5e400d0742dc087bf8d7771bdd4b/crates/victory-gpu/src/host.rs) resets its `fps, ms/frame, draws/frame` counters whenever ~**ONE second** elapsed, but **prints those results only about every five seconds**. Thus the older 3k+ draw `fps, ...ms/frame` lines are **sparse ~1s sampled averages**; they are **not averages over the full five-second logging interval**, and their quantiles are not true individual-frame percentiles. The new Run19 `each frame's time in the last N s` captures *every Present-to-Present interval* in each ~5s reporting period and should be used for elapsed-weighted native-presentation-rate/tail studies. For Run19 reporting periods with average >=3k draws: **2,611 intervals / 414.2s = 6.304/s**; **522 (20.0%) over250ms**, **5 >1s**, histogram `<34/50/75/100/150/200/300/500/1000/more = 763/196/72/42/121/253/994/138/27/5`. Pooled median interval lies in **150–200ms** bin, p90 **200–300ms**, p95 **300–500ms**, p99 **500–1000ms**. No exact pooled percentiles can be recovered from 10 bins; per-5s p99 can equal maximum for small N. This counts **real title Present calls, not FG**, but frame-content uniqueness is not independently instrumented.

**Multi-second stalls still dominate tail:** at **17:54:02** [Run19 log L39601] newest full-period report (13 presentations in 5.9s) has longest **1,951.6ms**, two >1s; GPU queue empty ~**1,027.7ms** in matching sampled frame (325.0ms while submitter working, most remainder title not submitting), texture preparation ~**194.5ms** / 813.6MB. At **17:54:08** [L39809], another **1,301.1ms** long interval; queue empty ~**1,184.4ms** and texture preparation ~197.1ms/722.5MB. At ~**17:45:39** a 3,467.9ms presentation interval occurs in very low-draw transition. These are **NOT DirectStorage >250ms requests**. GPU idle while *title not submitting* differs from GPU idle behind *submitter*, and neither by itself identifies the blocked CPU thread. R76 priority is an exact guest/submit/texture/fence cross-thread timeline. No GPU workload speedup can erase title-side submission gaps.

**Post-Run19 fork status (DO NOT REQUEST DUPLICATES):** Run19's [`920e6513`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/commit/920e6513bae997028cc2cae5d4f33f8510f7557d) already introduced complete per-present frame histogram, **bounded `XMemAllocatePhysicalPages` IDs** (`PAGE_LIMIT=1<<25`, free/reuse fallback), and kept-view write `Claim` for overlapping page-range writes. Run19 reports **114,096 page IDs given, 0 reused, 114,096 in use, highest `0x2BDAF0` of `0x2000000`**, so no reuse-path-on-game test or overflow occurred. Run19 reports **288,981 kept-view writes, 43,455 temporary mappings** from page-claim collisions (13.1% of counted writes), summing **439ms mapping/commit + 736ms unmapping** across 627.5s, while DStorage requests remained fast. **After Run19**, Claude already committed [`db95ed7`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/commit/db95ed7bdd87e5aaa0feb0e282f7f6eec274030c), which **claims only actual byte subranges** and lets non-overlapping bytes on one physical page use kept views simultaneously, with a source test. Its performance/correctness is **not in Run19** and requires Run20; Run19's 43,455 indicates same-page *ranges*, **NOT 43,455 proven same-byte conflicting writes**.

**Other correctness/quality risks unchanged:** [`draw.rs`](https://github.com/Zaxix/Defeat/blob/e992203c7abe5e400d0742dc087bf8d7771bdd4b/crates/victory-gpu/src/draw.rs) sampled warning changed-after-upload counter reaches **>=167,001**; new cumulative GPU-access category at last census: **165,766 'none'**, zero in read/read-write/unstated. `none` is the title's *declared guest GPU access permission*, not actual last-writer provenance or proof of a missed invalidation. Rechecking and uploading changed guest content may be expected after CPU writes; **do not silence warnings or assume 165k actual corrupt textures**. Run19 also preserves **26 Wave64 compute host refusals**, two known water shaders, guest shader-visible descriptor heap requested **1,245,184**, host bound **1,000,000** (out-of-range actual accesses not established); RT structure builder **OFF** and many 'dropped' items belong to that intentional omission. No captured matching image screenshots or DRED device removal present.

Identify title-not-submitted periods; do not blame disk requests without causal dependency.

- [ ] **R76/P0-C — Run20 safety/performance of Claude's already-merged byte-range `Claim`:** compare 43,455 Run19 page-collisions to byte-collisions in same scene, verify simultaneous distinct-byte writes, actual overlapping-byte fallback, early drop/failure, `Arc<Section>` lifetime, request status FIFO and old `=0` path. **Do not reimplement this code.**
- [ ] **R76/P0-D — Texture post-upload provenance:** trace 20 affected guest slots and section epochs, true CPU/DirectStorage/GPU last writer, last upload and fence, state changes and screenshot/image hashes; classify expected CPU changes vs alias invalidation faults.
